‘eṇṇaṉpa ēṉi eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum

kaṇṇaṉpa vāḻum uyirkku (kuṟaḷ—392)





eṇ eḻuttu ikaḻēl (ātticūṭi—7)





eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇeṉat takum



(koṉṟi vēntaṉ-7)





‘kaṟkak kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavi kaṟṟapiṉ

kiṟka ataṟkut takaveṉṟa-coṟkuḷḷē

ellārkkum putti iyampik kariyēṟṟa

vallāy uṉakku urikka vallēṉō’



(tamiḻviṭu tātu—97, 98)

kaṭanta aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷil 1930il kumārarājā (rājā) muttiyac ceṭṭiyār (nītikkaṭci) kalvip poṟuppil irunta nāḷ toṭṭu, iṉṟu (1991) māṇpumiku irāma. vīrappaṉ avarkaḷ attuṟiyiṉi vakikkum nāḷ variyil tamiḻakak kalvit tuṟiyiṉi — vaḷarcciyiṉi — māṟṟattiṉik kaṇṭavaṉātaliṉ — aṉṟu mutal iṉṟu vari ataṉoṭu toṭarpu uṭiyavaṉ ātaliṉ innūliṉi eḻuta muṟpaṭṭēṉ.

a.mu.pa.





muṉṉuri

kaṟka kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavi kaṟṟapiṉ

niṟka ataṟkut taka' eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar coṉṉa kuṟaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyilēyē inta nūl eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṟku viḷakkam tanta parimēlaḻakar ‘itaṉāl kaṟkappaṭum nūlkaḷum kaṟkumāṟum kaṟṟataṉāl payaṉum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa’ eṉkiṟār. inta muṟiyil iṉṟiya nāṭṭuk kalvi irukkiṟatā eṉpati nallōr eṇṇip pārkkavum illiyāṉāl vaḷḷuvar piṟanta nāṭṭil avar moḻinta vakiyil kalvittuṟiyi amikkavum vēṇṭiyē innūl eḻutappeṟṟatu.

nāṭṭil nām kaṟkiṟōm. āṉāl kaṟpavaṟṟi-kaṟka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟik kaṟkiṟōmā? illiyē! iṉṟiya kalvi makkaḷ vāḻkkikku-camutāya vaḷarccikku ēṟṟatāka uḷḷatā? ām eṉṟu yārāvatu kūṟamuṭiyumā ?

cari, avvāṟu kaṟpaṉavaṟṟik kacaṭuaṟa-piḻiyaṟā (pariti) mācuaṟa (kāḷiṅkar) kuṟṟamaṟak (maṇakkuṭavar) kaṟkiṟōmā? atuvum illiyē. ārampappaḷḷi mutal kallūri mutukali variyil avvāṟu māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟāl-kaṟkum vakiyil āciriyarkaḷ āṟṟuppaṭuttiṉāl tērvukaḷil nūṟṟukku nūṟu tēṟa vēṇṭāmā? āṉāl anta nili illiyē!

aṭuttu, kaṟṟapaṭiyāvatu niṟkiṟōmā? atuvum illiyē! ‘tām iṉpuṟuvatu ulaku iṉpuṟak kaṇṭu kāmuṟuvar kaṟṟaṟintār’ eṉṟa kuṟaḷukku ēṟpa, aṉivarum kaṟṟapaṭi naṭantāl nāṭṭil ‘ellārum ellāc celvamum’. peṟṟu iṉpamāka vāḻum nāṭākattāṉē itu irukkum. āṉāl anta iṉpam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?

eṉavē nāṭṭil tēviyāṉa-camutāya vāḻvi vaḷam paṭuttum kalvi illi eṉpatu kaṇ kūṭu. iti oruvāṟu viḷakkum vakiyilē ovvoru pakutiyum innūlil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu. akkuṟikaḷi nīkka vaḻikaḷum ōraḷavu cuṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

tirukkuṟaḷil poruṭpālil araciyal, toṭakkattē mutal atikāramāṉa iṟimāṭcikku (38) aṭutta atikāramākavē kalvi (89) amikiṉṟatu etik kāṭṭukiṟatu? kalviyi - tēviyāṉa kalviyit tam makkaḷukku aḷittalē araciṉ mutaṟ kaṭami eṉpatit tāṉē itu viḷakkukiṟatu. nāṭṭilē kalvi nalam peṟavilliyāṉāl, vēṟu etuvum nallamuṟiyil amiyātu eṉa aṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟu vari ellārum kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. aṉṟu aracukku aṟamuritta vaḷḷuvar toṭaṅki iṉṟu nam nāṭṭu-urimi nāṭṭup peruntalivar paṇṭita nēru uṭpaṭa aṉivarum ikkarutti valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉarē. 1961il iṉṟikku muppatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ intiya orumippāṭṭuk kuḻu amikka ēṟpāṭu ceyta mānāṭṭil paṇṭita nēru avarkaḷ, nāṭṭu orumippāṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭu vāḻa-nāṉilam vāḻa-camutāyam cīrpeṟak kalviyē mukkiyam eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. anta mānāṭṭil ellā araciyal kaṭcit talivarkaḷum mattiya amiccarkaḷum mānila mutal amiccarkaḷum, palkalik kaḻakat tuṇivēntarkaḷum kalvi vallunarkaḷum aṟiviyal mētikaḷum, toḻil mutalvarkaḷum kūṭi iruntaṉarē. (1961-cep 28 mutal ak 1 vari) anta mānāṭṭil kūṭi irunta attaṉi pērum iṉṟuvariyil kalviyil nalampeṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷ eṉṉa kaṇṭārkaḷ? eṇṇippārkka innūl cuṭar viḷakkāyiṉum naṉṟāy viḷakkiṭum tūṇṭukōlāka amikiṉṟatu. paṇṭitar nēru avarkaḷ kūṟiyatil oru pakutiyi iṅkē tarukiṟēṉ.

“Education provides the most important means of bringing about National integration. Since the Problems of National Integration or of national unity essentially, involue the attitudés of groups or large sections of the community and since Education in its brodest sense has been recognised as a powerful instrument for influencing or modifying these attitudes, the conference regards the process of Education and its re-orientation where necessary, as of primary importance” (The Hindu. 17. 11.91)

intak karuttiṉi avar vaḻiyē nāṭṭi āḷum mattiya aracō mānila aracukaḷō eṇṇip pārttu uṭaṉ tiruttiyirukka vēṇṭumē. iṉṟu, aṉṟu illāta vakiyil ettaṉiyō vēṟupāṭukaḷ muḷittut tali virittāṭukiṉṟaṉavē. ippōtum ittakiya pallōr kalanta nalam kāṇum kūṭṭaṅkaḷi iṉṟiya piratamarum naṭattukiṟār. ulaka vaṅki pōṉṟa paṉṉāṭṭu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ nam nāṭṭil kalvi-peṇ kalvi mika iḻi niliyil uḷḷatic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. (The Hindu- 22.11.91) eṉavē talivarkaḷ-aṟiñarkaḷ, kalvit tuṟiyiṉik kaṭṭi āḷupavarkaḷ paṇṭita nēru kūṟiyatiyum ulakattār paḻippatiyum eṇṇi uṭaṉ ceyalāṟṟa innūl oru tūṇṭukōlāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉptē eṉ āci.-- vēṇṭukōḷ.

ellāvaṟṟiṉum nallaṉavum uṇṭu; allaṉavum uṇṭu. eṉavē aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu anniyar āṭciyil irunta kalvi muṟiyiṉip paṟṟik kūṟi, atil uḷḷa nallaṉavaṟṟi eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉavum allaṉavaṟṟi viṭṭu vēṟu tiruttiya neṟikku māṟa vēṇṭumeṉavum nūlil aṅkaṅkē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. pala nallaṉa māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi eṇṇip pārkkavum vēṇṭiyuḷḷēṉ.

cutantiram peṟṟapiṉ mānila, mattiya aracukaḷ ikkalviyiṉ cīrtiruttam paṟṟiyum māṟṟam paṟṟiyum ettaṉiyō kuḻukkaḷi amittaṉa. aviyum palap pala vakikaḷ āyntu palvēṟu karuttukkaḷi ēṭṭil vaṭittut tantaṉa. āyiṉum avi ‘kūṭṭattil kūṭi niṉṟu kūṭip pitaṟṟal iṉṟi, nāṭṭattil koḷḷāraṭi-kiḷiyē nāḷil maṟappāraṭi’ eṉṟa pāratiyār vākkiṉ vaḻitāṉē niṟkiṉṟaṉa. aṇmiyil tiru. irāmamūrtti avarkaḷ talimiyil intiya aracu amitta kalvik kuḻuvum, nāṭṭu nalam kāṇattakka kalvi muṟiyiṉi ōraḷavu variyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. ataṉ karuttukkaḷ. cilavaṟṟiyum appaṭiyē innūlil , (āṅkilattil) cērttuḷḷēṉ.

atil, ōriṭattil kalviyil peṟa vēṇṭiya māṟṟaṅkaḷic cuṭṭi, avvāṟu nalla neṟiyil kalvi tiruttam peṟāviṭṭāl, attakiya kalviyik kaṟpatik kāṭṭilum kallātiruppatē mēl eṉavum kuṟittuḷḷār. (If this is what our education has done to us, one may well ask, is not no education better than bad education— Page V). itu eṉakku, ‘kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷ’ eṉṟa tāyumāṉavar aṭiyi niṉivūṭṭiṟṟu. tāyumāṉavar ‘kaṟṟum aṟivillāta eṉ kaṉmatti eṉ colluvēṉ’ eṉṟu nintu urukiyatiyum niṉikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟiya mattiya aracum mānila aracukaḷum inta neṟi vaḻiyip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum. pāṭaṅkaḷ cemmiyākkap peṟavēṇṭum. āciriyar paṭṭattukku ‘nallāciriyar’kaḷāka aṉṟi uṇmiyilēyē nallāciriyarkaḷākat tammiṭam payilvāri vāḻa vikkavēṇṭum. peṟṟōrkaḷum piṟavaṟṟik kāṭṭilum tam makkaḷ kaṇṇākiya kalviyil nalam peṟuvaḻiyē utavi ceyyavēṇṭum. māṇavarkaḷum iṉṟiya cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻalil cikkāmal kaṟpaṉa kaṟṟu, ataṉ vaḻi niṉṟu nāṭṭi vaḷamākka muyalavēṇṭum. intap pērāciyiṉ kāraṇamākavē nāṉ inta nūli eḻutiṉēṉ.

inta nūl nāṭṭukkalvi oṉṟiṉiyē nāṭṭamākak koṇṭu eḻutap peṟṟamiyiṉ atil taṟpōtu uḷḷa kuṟikaḷi aṅkaṅkē meṉmiyākavum vaṉmiyākavum cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. itaṉāl nāṉ yāriyum kuṟi kūṟiṉēṉ eṉṟō, kuṟittu matippiṭṭēṉ eṉṟō yārum eṇṇavēṇṭāmeṉak kēṭṭukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ.

eppaṭiyāyiṉum nammuṭiya paranta pāratamum ciṟanta tamiḻakamum paṇṭip perumikaḷit tirumpap peṟṟu, ulaka nāṭukaḷukkiṭiyil uyarnta oṉṟāka-piṟa nāṭukaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka āṉmīka neṟi, camutāyaneṟi. muṟipiṟaḻā aracaneṟi, kalvineṟi ākiya aṉittum mika uyarnta iṭattiṉip peṟṟuc ciṟakkavēṇṭum eṉṟa āci -kuṟikkōḷ aṭippaṭiyilēyē icciṟunūli veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ. nāṭṭiluḷḷa nallavar - vallavar-āṭciyāḷarkaḷ-aṟiñarkaḷ nallavaṟṟi ēṟṟu nāṭṭi vaḷañceytu kalviyiṉi nēriya vaḻiyil ompi ataṉ vaḻi nāṭṭu nalaṉik kāttu, nāṭu uyarntu ōṅka vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum eṉa vēṇṭi vaṇaṅki amikiṉṟēṉ.

tamiḻkkali illam

ceṉṉi-30 1.12.91

paṇivuḷḷa,

a.mu. paramacivāṉantam



* * *



acciṭumpōtu piḻikaḷi ottu nōkkit tirutti utaviya pērāciriyar tiru. cā. vaḷavaṉ avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟi urittu. .

a.mu.pa





﻿1. kalvi eṉum kaṇ



* * *



ulakam cuḻaṉṟu varum vēkattil makkaḷ uṇarvu ūcalāṭukiṉṟatu. ki.pi. 2000il itic ceyvōm, atic ceyvōm—nāṭṭi poṉṉāṭākkuvōm—viṇmuṭṭap pukaḻ parappuvōm eṉṟu mēli nāṭukaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi. paranta pāratamum ataṉ aṅkamāka uḷḷa tamiḻakamum nāḷtōṟum muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ceyal muṟiyil attakiya, nāṭṭamum ūkkamum uṇarvum vēkamum kāṭṭavilli:

"kūṭṭattil kūṭiniṉṟu kūṭippitaṟṟal iṉṟi

nāṭṭattil koḷḷāraṭi-kiḷiyē

nāḷil maṟappāraṭi"





eṉṟu pāratiyār inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil kūṟiya kūṟṟu. iṉṟum meymmiyāka aṉṟō uḷḷatu. ‘colvatu oṉṟu ceyvatu oṉṟu’ eṉpatu iṉṟiya vaḻakkamākiviṭṭatu. araciyalic cūtāṭṭam eṉpar. āṉāl aṟiviyal, āṉmaviyal, camūkaviyal, paṇpāṭṭiyal yāvumē cūtāṭṭamākavaṉṟō iṉṟu vaṭiveṭuttu ulavukiṉṟatu. pārati pāṭi oru nūṟṟāṇṭu-avaṉ maṟintu mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭu, urimi peṟṟu ari nūṟṟāṇṭu ippaṭi nūṟṟāṇṭu elli kaḻintum intiya makkaḷ—tamiḻ makkaḷ entavakiyil muṉṉēṟi uḷḷaṉar eṉṟu colla muṭiyum? eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ!

‘vañcamiṉṟi vāḻumiṉ’ eṉṟu periyavarkaḷ valiyuṟuttiya nāṭṭil-vaḷḷuvar piṟanta nāṭṭil vāḻkki neṟi eppaṭi varampiṉṟi vīḻntu, nāṭṭiyum ulakiyum nalivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu palar pēcuvatu

pōṉṟu 'āṅkilēyaṉ āṭciyē mēl' eṉṟa uṇarvu tāṉē palarukku uṇṭākiṉṟatu. uṇavilē, uṭiyilē, ūrp payaṇattilē, uṟṟa ceyalkaḷilē maṉitaṉ māṟi viṭṭālum kavali illi. kālam avaṉit tirutti viṭum eṉa nampalām. āṉāl uḷḷattāl-uḷḷat tuṇarntu kāṇum paṇpāṭṭāl-appaṇpāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē mukiḻkkum ceyalāl avaṉ māṟiviṭṭāl avaṉi eṉṟum yārālum tirutta muṭiyātē! camutāyattil, taṉṉi oru aṅkamākkikkoṇṭu, tāṉ camutāyattukkāka vāḻavēṇṭiyatu makkaḷ niyati. nām camutāyattukku-uyiriṉattukku iṉṟu eṉṉa ceytōm eṉa avaṉ iravil paṭukkap pōkumpōtu eṇṇip pārkkak kaṭamippaṭṭavaṉ. āṉāl iṉṟiya maṉitaṉ, eṉakku maṟṟavar iṉṟu eṉṉa ceytārkaḷ-nāṉ yār yāriṭam kiyūṭṭu vāṅkiṉēṉ-mottam evvaḷavu āyiṟṟu-ēṉ? ettaṉi pērukkut tīṅkiḻittēṉettaṉi pērik koli ceytēṉ eṉṟallavā kaṇakkiṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. pōrkkaḷattil kaḷam kāṇavēṇṭiya-ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭiya paṭikkalaṉkaḷ, iṉṟu vīṭutoṟum-terutoṟum-cantitoṟum ōyātu olippatiyum pāḻpaṭuttuvatiyum nām kāṇkiṟōm. 'tuppākki kalācāram' (Gun Culture) eṉṟu 'piṟaric cuṭutalākiya paṇpāṭē' eṉṟu pēcuvataṉṟō iṉṟu nāṭṭil naṭamāṭukiṉṟatu. māṇavaṉ āciriyarik kaṇṭu payantu-paṇintu vaṇaṅkiya kālam pōy āciriyar māṇavarik kaṇṭu payantu naṭuṅkum kālam vantuviṭṭatu. nāṭāḷum maṉṉarkaḷ accamiṉṟi iravilum. pakalilum taṉiyāka nāṭṭu nalaṉ kāṇa varumpōtu makkaḷ añciyum aṉpuḷattālum pōṟṟi vaṇaṅkiya nilimāṟi, āḷukiṉṟavarkaḷ makkaḷukku añci aṭukkaṭukkākak kāval cūḻa aṉṟō veḷi varavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. vīṭutōṟum kaliyiṉ viḷakkam eṉṟu kēṭṭavarkaḷukkellām putuppaḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷ tiṟakka vāri vaḻaṅkiya paṇpāṭu nīṅki, iṉṟu

paḷḷiyō kallūriyō toṭaṅkavō aṉṟip putupputup pāṭaṅkaḷ peṟavō ilaṭcakkaṇakkākak kiyūṭṭu taravēṇṭiya kālamākavaṉṟō iṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. inta nāṭṭil lañcam tāṇṭavamāṭāta iṭam eṅkum illi eṉṟa perumikku iṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ vaḻivakuttu uḷḷārkaḷē. ciṟu ūḻiyarkaḷ pattu, nūṟu eṉṟa aḷavil niṟka, periyavarkaḷ-uyarniliyāḷarkaḷ, āṭciyāḷarkaḷ ilaṭcakkaṇakkil kōṭikkaṇakkil kiyūṭṭu vāṅkuvatutāṉē iṉṟiya vāḻkki neṟi, ‘campaḷam kimpaḷam’ eṉṟu vēṭikkikkākac collum coṟṟoṭar iṉṟu vāḻkkiyil mukkiya iṭam peṟṟu viṭṭatē. paḷḷiyilum kallūriyilum cērppataṟku āyirak kaṇakkilum ilaṭcak kaṇakkilum vārikkoṭṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. appaṭiyē vēli peṟat takuti vēṇṭātu lañcamē aḷavu kōlāka amiya ilaṭcattiṉ elliyiṉiyum atu kaṭakkiṉṟatē!-inta avala niliyil, ‘maṉitaṉ eṅkē celkiṟāṉ?’ eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatē inta niliyil pārati kūṟiyapaṭi ‘nāṭṭattil koḷvatu’ eṅkē? eppaṭi muṭiyum?

pala poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi eṇṇam eḻukiṟatu. iṅkē mutalil kalviyip paṟṟik kāṇalām. paḻaṅkālattil kalvi pōṟṟap peṟṟami ‘kurukula vācam’ eṉṟa vakiyāl nām aṟikiṟōm. taṉitta kuruviṉiṭm, avarkaḷ vīṭṭup paṇikaḷiyum ceytu koṇṭu, kuṭumpamākac celvarum vaṟiyarum vēṟupāṭu iṉṟi, kalvi kaṟṟu muṉṉēṟi nāṭṭi vāḻvittaṉar. vaṟiñarāṉa kucēlarum araca mintaṉāṉa kaṇṇaṉum orē kuruviṭam vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kaṟṟaṉar. pala pēraṟiñarkaḷ araca kumārarkaḷiyum āṇṭikaḷiṉ piḷḷikaḷiyum tammuṭaṉ irutti, takka vakiyil kalvi kaṟpittatip paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl iṉṟu anta niliyilli. āciriyarkaḷum naṉṉūlāciriyar kāṭṭiya nallāciriyarkaḷ pōṉṟu kōlṭu smit eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar kāṭṭiya eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar kāṭṭiya :kirāmap paḷḷi āciriyar pōṉṟu muṟṟum kaṟṟu uṇarntavarkaḷāy-ūrukku vaḻikāṭṭum uttamarāy, etuvariṉum kalaṅkāta uḷa uram uṭiyavarāy-taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷarāy-tali nimirntu maliyiṉum māṇāp paṇpu peṟṟa periyavarāy vāḻntuvantaṉar. āṉāl iṉṟiya āciriyarkaḷ niliyiṉi eṇṇip pārkkak kūṭavilli. eṅkō oru cilar paḻiya paṇpāṭṭu neṟi māṟāmal vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum perumpālāṉa āciriyarkaḷ tāḻvuṟṟut taṟikeṭṭuttāṉē vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi māṇavar naṭantāl eṉṉākum? paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvatilum ettaṉi ēmāṟṟam. pala palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ kāḷāṉkaḷākat tōṉṟi-aracu tarum urimam iṉṟiyē (aṅkīkāramiṉṟiyē) ceyalpaṭṭu, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷi īrttu, piṉ naṭutteruvil. niṟkaviṭukiṉṟaṉa. rupāy irunūṟum muṉṉūṟum tantāl ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟa vāyppum uṇṭē! ivi paṟṟiyellām nāḷtōṟum nāḷitaḻkaḷil ceytikaḷik kāṇkiṉṟōmē! paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvatippaṟṟiyum nakippukkiṭamāṉa vakiyil pala ceytikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉavē. ‘ṭākṭar’ paṭṭam peṟuvataṟkup paṇamē mukkiyam. paṭippu mukkiyamilli eṉṟu cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ oru ciṟanta nāḷitaḻil aṉṉiyār oruvar eḻutiya kaṭṭuri eṉ niṉivukku varukiṉṟatu. ati yārāvatu -entap palkalik kaḻakamāvatu maṟuttatuṇṭā? uṇmiyi eppaṭi maṟukkamuṭiyum?

mēli nāṭukaḷil ciṟappāka amerikkāvil tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻaṅkālak kalvi muṟiyiṉi, nāṉ cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceṉṟapōtu: kaṇṭu makiḻntēṉ. eṉ nūlilum (ēḻu nāṭukaḷil, eḻupatu nāṭkaḷ) atupaṟṟik kuṟittuḷḷēṉ. orē pāṭattukku. nāṉku āciriyar eṉṟa avala nili aṅkilli. orē pērāciriyariṉ kīḻ, orē

teḷinta pāṭattiṉi eṭuttuk koṇṭu, avariṭamē muṟiyākap payiṉṟu, nūl niliyattiṉi naṉku payaṉpaṭutti, takka vakiyil cila viṭaṅkaḷil āṇṭu elli kūṭa iṉṟip paṭṭam peṟṟu veḷiyēṟukiṉṟaṉar. avartam teḷinta pulamiyi ulakam viyantu pōṟṟukiṉṟatu. itu namnāṭṭup paḻakkālak kurukula vākkiyiṉiyē niṉivūṭṭukiṟataṉṟō! aṅkē tamiḻum anta vakiyilēyē payiṟṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘oḷaviyār’ pāṭalkaḷ oṉṟaṉiyē pāṭamākap peṟṟu, orē āciriyariṉ kīḻp paṭṭam peṟap payiṉṟavari nāṉ kaṇṭēṉ. appaṭiyē ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟuvatum āḻntu payiṉṟu teḷintālaṉṟi iyalātu. peṉcilvēṉiyā palkalikkaḻakattē tamiḻnāṭṭup poruḷ paṟṟiya (worship of Sun God in Tamilnadu during nineth century) kaṭṭuri ‘em.e.’ vakuppiṟku āyvuk kaṭṭuriyāka amiya, pala tērvāḷarkaḷōṭu nāṉum oruvarāy iruntēṉ. nila nūl, tamiḻ, vāṉanūl pōṉṟa pala nūlkaḷ kaṟṟa pala tuṟiyiṉar 36 pēr kūṭṭi, antakkaṭṭuriyi ārāyntu, kēṭṭa kēḷvikḷukkellām. anta māṇavi patil aḷittār. ciṟanta vakiyil amintatāl paṭṭamum peṟṟār.

iṅkē ṭākṭar paṭṭat tērviṟku ammāṇavariṉ mēṟpārviyāḷarē mukkiya tērvāḷar. avarukku vēṇṭiya vēṟu iruvar uṭaṉ tērvāḷarkaḷ. piṉ eti eḻutiṉum paṭṭam peṟat taṭi eṉṉa?

eṅkaḷ kallūriyil, tamiḻil mutal vakuppil tēṟiya oruvar tamiḻāciriyarāka iruntār. oru nāḷ avari aḻittu, ‘vākkuṇṭām’ eṉṟa nūlip paṟṟi ētēṉum teriyumā eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. āmām ‘vākkuṇṭām nalla maṉmuṇṭām’ eṉṟa pāṭal irukkiṟatu eṉṟu yōcittuc coṉṉār. āṉāl anta nūlip paṟṟiyō ataṉ āciriyarip paṟṟiyō avr eḻutiya vēṟu nūlkaḷip

paṟṟiyō oṉṟum teriyavilli. nallavēḷi avarkaḷ ippōtu paṇiyil illi.

orumuṟi tērvāṇiyattil kallūri āciriyarit tērvu ceyyum paṇikku aḻikkappeṟṟēṉ. aṅkē vanta oruvar ‘em.ē.’ vakuppil civa ilakkiyaṅkaḷic ciṟappup pāṭamāka eṭuttiruntār. eṉavē nāṉ avarkaḷi nōkki, mutal mūṉṟu tirumuṟikaḷip pāṭiyavar yār? eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. uṭaṉē tayakkamillāmal ‘tirunāvukkaracar’ eṉṟār. uṟuppiṉar aṉivarum cirittaṉar. eṉ pakkattilirunta mukamatiya uṟuppiṉar ‘aiyā, eṉakkut terikiṟatē, ivarkaḷukkut teriya villiyē’ eṉṟu varuntiṉār. anta āṇṭu avarit tērvu ceyyavilli. eṉiṉum aṭutta āṇṭu eppaṭiyō iṭampiṭittu, ippoḻutu oru kallūriyil pērāciriyarāka irukkiṟār. itupōṉṟu ettaṉiyō cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭa muṭiyum.

tamiḻil maṭṭumiṉṟi piṟamoḻikaḷilum piṟapāṭaṅkaḷilum itē niliyiṉik kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. eṅkaḷ kallūri, paḷḷikaḷukku varum viṇṇappaṅkaḷip pārttāl mayakkam vantuviṭum. āṅkilattil ciṟappu niliyil 80% mēl eṇ vāṅkiya oruvar eḻutiya viṇṇappattil kuṟintatu aintāṟu cātāraṇap piḻikaḷ irukkum. appaṭiyē tamiḻ payiṉṟavarkaḷ nērmukat tērvil caṅkap pāṭalkaḷil cātāraṇa kēḷvikaḷ kēṭkum pōtukūṭa, patil colla muṭiyātu tiṇaṟuvārkaḷ ittakiya āciriyarkaḷiṭam pāṭam payilum māṇavarkaḷ eppaṭi vallunarkaḷāka-nāṭṭiṉ perumiyik kaṭṭikkāppavarāka varamuṭiyum? nāṭāḷum nallavar inta niliyiṉi eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum.

nāṭṭil poṟiyiyal kallūri, maruttuvak kallūri pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām nūṟṟukku 70, allatu 80 vāṅka

vēṇṭum eṉa variyaṟukkiṟārkaḷ, ‘pi.pi.ē.’ pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷukku 60% tēviyākiṟatu. āṉāl āciriyar paṭippukkō oṉṟum tēvi illi. pala muṟi tōlviyuṟṟu nūṟṟukku muppattintu nāṟpatu vāṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟa viti uḷḷatu. ‘itu muṟiyō! itu takumō! itu tarumantāṉō?’ eṉṟu vaḷḷalārip pōṉṟu vāy viṭṭu alaṟa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.

āciriyar payiṟci ciṟuvakuppukaḷukku uriya āciriyarkaḷukku (Sec-Grade) iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ irukka, uyarnilip paḷḷiyil payiṟṟuvōrukku ōrāṇṭākak kuṟittatu ēṉō eppaṭi anta elliyil avarkaḷ payiṟci peṟa muṭiyum? avarkaḷukku (Practical work) kaḷappaṇiyum cemmiyāka naṭipeṟuvatilli. jūli kaṭiciyil cērntu, pipravari iṟutiyil muṭittu. mārccil tērvu eḻuti, paṭṭam peṟṟavar eppaṭi mutirnta aṟivuṭiya pavaṇanti kāṭṭiya āciriyarāka muṭiyum? atilum ippōtu añcal vaḻikkalvi muṟiyilē ippayiṟciyip palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭṭup pukuttiyuḷḷaṉa. eppaṭi, kaḷappaṇiyum pāṭaṅkaḷ aṟiyum niliyum avarkaḷukku amiyum. ākavē eppaṭiyō iyantira cāliyil iyantiraṅkaḷi uṟpatti ceytu aṉuppuvatu pōṉṟu palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ pallāyiravari aṉuppi nāṭṭip pāḻpaṭuttukiṟatu. iyantiraṅkaḷāvatu cemmiyākac ceyalāṟṟum. āṉāl āciriyarkaḷ? ivarkaḷ tērviṉit takka vakiyil 70% mēṟpaṭṭa eṇ peṟṟavarākat tērnteṭuttu, iraṇṭāṇṭu takka āciriyariṉ kīḻp payiṟci peṟac ceytāl ivarkaḷiṭam payilvōr ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvar. inta aṭippaṭi illi. nāṭṭil kalviyiṉ taram ‘kuṟintu viṭṭatu-kuṟintu viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu mattiya amiccarkaḷ-mānila mutalvarkaḷ ākiyōr kūṟuvatil payaṉilli. cikareṭṭu viḷamparatti aḻakāka nāḷitaḻil viḷamparappaṭutti, aṭiyil cikareṭṭu piṭippatu nōyaṟṟa vāḻvukkum kēṭu payappatu’ eṉṟu acciṭa vēṇṭum eṉa: aracāṅkam caṭṭam ceytuḷḷatu. cikareṭṭi aracāṅkam virumpiṉāl taṭi ceyyalām. atic ceyyātu, atu paṟṟi viḷamparaṅkaḷiyum aṉumatittu, ataṉ kīḻē atu tīṅku ceyyak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu acciṭuvatu kēlik kūttāka allavō uḷḷatu. nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉivarum muṭṭāḷkaḷ eṉak karutukiṟārkaḷō eṉavum niṉikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. eṉavē inta vakiyil nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇāṉa kalviyiṉik kāṭṭum nallāciriyarkaḷit tērnteṭukkum mutal paṇi cemmiyāka naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum. attakiya nili amintāl-nallavar-kaṟṟavar-vallavar-kaṭami uṇarntu ceyalāṟṟupavar (ki eḻuttiṭṭu vēṟu vēli kavaṉikkātu ceyalpuripavar) māṇavar nalam karuti avar uḷaṅkoḷap pāṭam colpavar pavaṇantiyiṉ ilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamāka amipavar āciriyarkaḷāka amivar. avvāṟu amintāl, nāṭṭuk kalvi uyarnili peṟum eṉpatu uṟuti. āṭciyāḷarkaḷ itil mutal kavaṉam-mukkiya kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.

palkalik kaḻakamāṉiyak kuḻu, inta añcal vaḻikkalvi muṟiyiṉi-āciriyar payiṟci aḷavil eṭuttuviṭap pōvatāka aṇmiyil oru ceyti vantāka aṟikiṟēṉ: virivil ceyalākkap peṟiṉ payaṉ viḷiyum.

maṟṟoru vēṭikki. kallūri āciriyarukku" entavitap payiṟciyum illi. nēṟṟuvari ‘em.ē.’, payiṉṟavar em.pil., tērvu eḻutiyavar, iṉṟu cūṉ tiṅkaḷil anta ‘em.ē’ allatu ‘pi.ē’. vakuppiṟkup pāṭam kaṟpikkum āciriyarākiviṭalām. iti enta aṟivuḷḷa ulakamāvatu ēṟṟukkoḷḷumā? eṇṇippārkka vēṇṭāmā? piṉ evvāṟu nam māṇavarkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum? iṉiyākilum uḷḷa āciriyap payiṟcik kallūriyilō aṉṟip putitākak kallūri amittō taṉiyākak kallūri āciriyarkaḷukkup payiṟciyiṉi aḷittal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum, paḷḷikaḷum kallūrikaḷum nāṭṭil palkip peruki. viṭṭaṉa. āṉāl taram tāḻntuviṭṭatu. itutāṉ āḷum talivarkaḷ uṭpaṭap palarum kūṟuvatu. ēṉ inta avalanili? nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiya iraṇṭoṉṟu maṭṭum kāraṇamāka niṉṟuviṭātu. iṉṉum cila uḷḷaṉa.

iṉṟu ellārum kalvi kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟa avāviṉāl palarum tam kuḻantikaḷip paḷḷikku aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl nakaraṅkaḷilum - kirāmaṅkaḷilum kūṭa āṅkilp payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷē atikamāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. ‘vīti’tōṟum iraṇṭoru paḷḷi eṉṟu pārati ittakiya paḷḷikaḷic collavilli. vīṭṭil peṟṟōrkaḷum mammi, ṭāṭi eṉṟu kuḻantikaḷ aḻippatil perumi koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. āyirakkaṇakkil ilañcam koṭuttu, nūṟṟukkaṇakkil mātac campaḷam kaṭṭi, nerukkamāṉa vīṭukaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa ippaḷḷikaḷil peṟṟōr tam kuḻantikaḷic cērkkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē uriya pāṭaṅkaḷiyum naṉku kaṟṟut taruvatilli. eṅkō oru cila paḷḷikaḷ tavira, pala, vaṇika niliyaṅkaḷākavē ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟit taṭukka aracāṅkam takka naṭavaṭikki eṭukka vēṇṭum. attakiya paḷḷikaḷil payiṉṟa piḷḷikaḷi ekkāraṇam koṇṭum, aracāṅka icivu peṟṟa paḷḷikaḷil cērkkak kūṭātu eṉṟa uttiraviṭṭu, atic cemmiyākap pātukākka vēṇṭum. ‘mutaṟkōṇal muṟṟum kōṇal’ eṉṟapaṭi inta mutaṟ kōṇalē nāṭṭuk kalviyi nalivu ceykiṟatu. itu māṟṟap peṟal vēṇṭum. aracāṅkam uṭaṉ āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.

paḷḷiyil payilum māṇavarum āciriyarum oruvari oruvar purintukoḷḷum vakiyil pāṭaṅkaḷ amivatōṭu avaṟṟi māṇavar naṉku aṟintu teḷinta piṉṉarē mēl vakuppiṟku māṟṟap peṟal vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil cērntuviṭṭāl pattām vakuppu vari, taṉṉi yārum taṭi ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṟa niṉivil palar paṭikkāmalum paḷḷikkup pala nāḷ varāmalum vēṟu vakiyil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cila peṟṟōr taṅkaḷ vēlikaḷukkup piḷḷikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.

ovvoru vakuppilum tērviṉi muṟiyāka naṭatti naṉku eḻuti veṟṟi peṟupavariyē mēl vakuppiṟku aṉuppa vēṇṭum, entavitat tērvum iṉṟip pattām vakuppu variyil cellalām eṉṟa nili orukāl iruntatu. nūṟṟukkup patiṉintu peṟṟāl pōtum; mēl vakuppiṟkup pōkalām eṉṟa niyatiyum orukāl iruntatu. iṉṟu avi illi eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. paḷḷiyil payilum piḷḷikaḷ vakuppiṟku ēṟṟa pāṭaṅkaḷi muṟiyākap payiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāka mēlēṟiṉāl tāṉ payaṉ uṇṭu. inta nili naṉku vaṟpuṟuttappaṭa vēṇṭum.

āṇṭutōṟum āciriyar nāḷ (cep-5) naṭipeṟukiṟatu tērccipeṟṟa ciṟanta māṇavarkaḷukkup paricuvaḻaṅkap peṟukiṟatu. nallāciriyar virutum vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘nallāciriyar’ virutu paṟṟic cilarukku maṉakkacappu uṇṭu. iṅkē nāṉ atupaṟṟi ārāyavirumpavilli. āṉāl māṇavarukkup paricu vaḻaṅkuvatu eṇṇattakkatu. pala paḷḷikaḷil māṇavar taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ paḷḷikaḷilēyē tērvu eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. pala paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavarkaḷ vēṟupaḷḷikaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ kāraṇatti nāṉ ārāya vēṇṭā. āṉāl tam paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutum māṇavarkaḷukku āciriyarkaḷ tāmē viṭicollit taruvatum, nūlkaḷit tantu viṭi eḻuta vippatum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl atē paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutum vēṟṟuppaḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku viṉāttāḷum uriya vēḷiyil taruvatilli muṭiyap pattu nimiṭam irukkum pōtu, eḻutat tāḷkēṭṭāl, ‘nī iṉi eṉṉa eḻutik kiḻikkappōkiṟāy! pōtum koṭu’ eṉṟu taṭuppavarum uḷḷaṉar. itupōṉṟa koṭumikaḷpaṟṟit tērvāṇiyarukkum peṟṟōr kaṭitam eḻuti iruppārkaḷ.

muṉpellām oru paḷḷimāṇavar maṟṟoru paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutuvatu

vaḻakkam. tiru vi ka. paḷḷi kiṟittuvar kallūrip paḷḷiyil eḻutalām. kiṟittuvar kallūrip paḷḷi vēṟupaḷḷiyil eḻutalām. vēṟu paḷḷi māṇavar tiru.vi.ka. vil eḻutalām. inta muṟi kallūrikkum poruntum. nāṉ pacciyapparil paṇiyāṟṟiya ñāṉṟu perumpālum layōlā kallūri māṇavar pacciyapparil eḻutuvar.

inta muṟi iruppiṉ māṇavar uṇmiyāṉa tiṟaṉ kaṇṭu teḷiyap peṟum. illiyāyiṉ iṉṟu ciṟanta māṇavarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum parici ‘lañcamaṟṟu’ vaḻaṅkiya paricākakkoḷḷa iyalātu. mattiya kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil vēṟu paḷḷiyiṉ talivar vantu paḷḷiyiṉ tērvi naṭattuvar. ataṉiṉum muntiyatu ciṟantatu. vēṇṭumāyiṉ māṇavar aṭiyāḷam kāṭṭa, payiṉṟa paḷḷiyiṉ iraṇṭoru āciriyar tērvuppaḷḷiyil kaṇkāṇippāḷarākac cellalām. oru kallūriyil mūṉṟumaṇi nērat tāḷi nāṉkumaṇi nēram eḻutavittatōṭu viṭikaḷiyum āciriyarkaḷ collikkoṭuttārkaḷām. eppōtō oru muṟi palkalik kaḻakap paṟakkumpaṭi varum. appōtu caṟṟu nimirntu viṭuvārkaḷām. itupaṟṟic ceṉṟa āṇṭu palkalikkaḻakattukkuk kuṟippuvantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl palkalikkaḻakam eṉṉa naṭavaṭikki eṭuttatō teriyātu. eṉavē, tērvumuṟi intavakiyil māṟṟi amittālaṉṟi, ciṟanta māṇavarukkup paricu vaḻaṅkal oru kēlikkūttākavē muṭiyum.

iṉi paḷḷinaṭakkum vēḷiyiṉippaṟṟi eṇṇuvōm. mēlināṭukaḷilum amerikkā jappāṉ malēciyā pōṉṟa kīḻināṭukaḷilum paḷḷikaḷ kāli 7.30 allatu 8kkuḷ toṭaṅki 12 allatu 1 maṇikkuḷ muṭintuviṭum. kāli 8 maṇikkuc cūriyaṉik kāṇāta anta nāṭukaḷilellām avvāṟu viṭiyalil paḷḷikaḷ naṭakkumpōtu, namnāṭṭil maṭṭum pattukku eṉa vittu, naṉku uṇṭapiṟaku, uṟaṅkum niliyil paḷḷikkup piḷḷikaḷi aṉuppukiṟōm. ceṉṉi pōṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷ tāy, tantiyar iruvarum vēlikkup pōvatāl perumpālum avarkaḷpaṇi 10kkut toṭaṅkuvatāl kuḻantikaḷi appaṭiyē paḷḷiyil viṭṭu vēlikkuc cella ceḷakariyamākum eṉa niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. nallavēḷiyāka, pas nerukkaṭi kāraṇamākac ceṉṟa āṇṭu kāli 8.30kkup paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅka muṭivu ceytu iṉṟu naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷatu. inta nili toṭarntu naṭipeṟutal nallatu. .

‘veḷḷi muḷikkap paḷḷikkum vārum’ eṉṟu vināyakar vaḻipāṭṭil ōraṭi varukiṟatu. appaṭi viṭiyaṟkāliyil ceṉṟu. viḷakkiṉṟēṉum muṟiyam collum vakiyil, pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷi, vāypāṭukaḷi muṟiyāka āciriyarō, maṟṟiya aṟinta māṇavarō colla, maṟṟavarkaḷ colluvārkaḷ. piṉ poḻutu viṭiya; avaravar vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, oṉpatu maṇi aḷavil vantu pāṭaṅkaḷip payilvar. inta ‘muṟiyam’ collum vakiyil kīḻvāy ilakkam, mutaliya kaṇakkukkuriya vāypāṭukaḷ maṉappāṭamākiṉṟamiyāl iṉṟiya (Clerk Table) kiḷārkṭēpil pōṉṟavi illāmalē ettakiya. piṉṉa kaṇakkukaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum maṉattālēyē pōṭṭu muṭivu eṭuppar. appaṭiyē nikaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa paṭippatāl, akarāti iṉṟiyē ellā ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum uri kāṇpar. nalla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ maṉappāṭam āvatāl avartam pēccum eḻuttum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum. iḷamiyil eṉ kirāmattil, akkālattil (1918-24) ivvāṟu payiṉṟami iṉṟum niṉivukku varukiṟatu. āyiṉum inta muṟi iṉṟu naṭipeṟātu. atupōṉṟu viṭiyalil eḻuntu collittarum āciriyarum illi; māṇvarkaḷum eḻuntu pōka māṭṭārkaḷ. ōraḷavu iṉṟu vēṟupala cātaṉaṅkaḷ vantuviṭṭamiyāl maṉappāṭam tēvi illāta oṉṟāki viṭṭatu maṉitaṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇippoṟi maṉitaṉik kāṭṭilum āyiramāyiram niṉivāṟṟal uṭiyatāka, maṉitaṉuṭiya vāḻviyē taṉatākkik koḷḷum niliyil nām vāḻkiṟōm. eṉavē kurukula vācamum maṉappāṭam ceyyum muṟiyum mella mella illiyākiviṭṭaṉa.

iṉi, pāṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟi ōraḷavu eṇṇalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.



kaṇitam paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭu payilvar piṉ kārkoḷvāṉilōr mīṉ nilitērntilār





aṇicey kāviyam āyiram kaṟkiṉum āḻntirukkum kaviyuḷam kāṇkilār





vaṇikamum poruḷ nūlum pitaṟṟuvār vāḻum nāṭṭil poruḷkeṭal kēṭṭilār





tuṇiyum āyiram cāttira nāmaṅkaḷ colluvār eṭṭuṇip payaṉkaṇṭilār





eṉṟupārati pāṭiya atē niliyiltāṉē iṉṟum nāṭu irukkiṟatu. 11+2+2 eṉpatumāṟi 11+1+3 eṉamāṟiṟṟu piṉ atuvum māṟi 10+2+3 eṉa māṟiṟṟu. ippaṭi varuṭak kaṇakku māṟi māṟi varuvatāl maṭṭum kalviyiṉ taram uyarntu viṭumā? pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷil eṉṉa māṟṟam uṇṭu? appaṭiyē tamiḻ iraṇṭām iṭattiliruntu mutaliṭam eṉṟu peyarip peṟṟatē tavira āṅkilamtāṉ kaṭṭāyam payila vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻō piṟa intiya moḻikaḷōṭu, maṟṟiya pirañcu, lattīṉ moḻikaḷōṭu oru moḻiyākavē otukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil tiru. aviṉāciliṅkam ceṭṭiyār kalvi amiccarāka iruntapōtu, iṉṟikkuc cumār nāṟpatāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil payilum māṇavar aṉivarum tamiḻik kaṭṭāyamākap paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. caṭṭam ceytār. aṉṟu pāṭa nūlkaḷit taṉiyār veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. eṉiṉum attakiya pāṭanūlkaḷi uṭaṉ tuṇintu eḻuta yārum muṉ varavilli immuṟi eṉṉākumō? eṉa añciṉar. amiccar eṉṉi aḻittu eḻutaccoṉṉārkaḷ. nāṉ pāṭanūl eḻutuvatu illi eṉṟa koḷki uṭiyavaṉ. eṉiṉum avar kēṭṭukkoṇṭataṟkāka icintu āṟu, ēḻu. eṭṭu ākiya mūṉṟu vakuppukaḷukkup pāṭanūl eḻutiṉēṉ. ataṉi veḷiyiṭat tamiḻnāṭṭup patippakaṅkaḷ virintu ceyalpaṭātapōtu, appōtu āṅkilap patippakamāka irunta mākmillaṉ kampeṉiyē veḷiyiṭṭatu. piṉṉāl palarum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.

tamiḻiṉip potu, ciṟappu eṉa iruvakiyākap pirittu, potuviṉi ellārum payilavēṇṭum eṉavum ciṟappiṉukkup patilāka ciṟupāṉmiyōr avaravar tāy moḻiyiṉiyō vēṟu vēṇṭiya moḻiyiṉiyō kaṟkalām eṉavum vitittu avvāṟē oru cila āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻ kaṭṭāyamāka iruntatu. atē vēḷiyil vēṟṟu moḻiyāḷarākiya ciṟupāṉmiyōrukkum avaravar moḻiyiṉik kaṟka vāyppum iruntatu. āyiṉum atē kāṅkiraciṉic cērnta tiru. cuppiramaṇiyam kalviyamiccarāka vantatum, anta muṟiyi eṭuttuviṭṭu, tamiḻip patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷōṭu oṉṟākkiṉār. aṉṟumutal varum kalvi amiccarkaḷ mutalvarkaḷuṭaṉ itic ceyalākkavēṇṭum; tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻik kaṭṭāyamākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭum yārum cevicāykkavilli. aṇṭi mānilaṅkaḷil kaṉṉaṭam, marātti pōṉṟa moḻikaḷ kaṭṭāyamāka vantu ettaṉiyō āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. nalla vēḷi iṉṟiya mutalvarum kalvi amiccarum virivil tamiḻik kaṭṭāyam payila vaḻikāṇa ēṟpāṭu ceyvatāka

aṟiya āṟutal piṟakkiṟatu. avarkaḷi vāḻttiyum pārāṭṭiyum kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutiṉēṉ. virintu ceyvārkaḷ eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. aṭutta kalviyāṇṭilēyē (1992-93) atu ceyalākkappeṟum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ.

pāṭanūlkaḷi iṉṟu aracāṅkamē veḷiyiṭukiṟatu. anta veḷiyīṭṭakam periya pattu māṭik kaṭṭaṭamum kaṭṭikkoṇṭu ciṟakka vāḻkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum pāṭaṅkaḷip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷukkum māṇavarkaḷukkum nalla nūlkaḷit tērnteṭukkum vāyppu illi. virumpiṉālum virumpāviṭṭālum orē vakiyāṉa nūlittāṉ paṭikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. muṉpellām-kālnūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ pala patippāḷarkaḷ-nūṟṟukku mēl iruppar eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. moḻippāṭaṅkaḷ uṭpaṭa nalla pāṭanūlkaḷi eḻutiṉārkaḷ-veḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. pala teḷinta aṟivum matinuṭpamum uṭiya āciriyarkaḷ nūlkaḷ eḻuta vāyppu iruntatu. appaṭiyē patippakattārum taramāṉa nūlkaḷi veḷikkoṇara muṭintatu. avaṟṟi ārāya ovvoru pāṭattiṟkum valluṉarkuḻukkaḷ amittu, nallaṉavaṟṟik koṇṭu, allaṉavaṟṟit taḷḷiṉar. paḷḷikaḷilum nallaṉavaṟṟi ārāynteṭuttu, ciṟakkak kalviyiṉi-ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum kaṟṟut tantaṉar. ippōtō āḷum kaṭcikku vēṇṭiyavarkaḷi āciriyar kuḻukkaḷāka amittu avarkaḷ tērnteṭukkum pakutikaḷi-cilacamayam araciyal vāṭi vīcuvaṉavāka uḷḷavaṟṟiyum tokuttup pāṭanūlkaḷākat tarukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam pāṭattiṭṭattiṉiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum cariyākap purintu koḷḷātu ētō tokuttōm eṉṟu pāṭanūlkaḷit tokuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. putiya pāṭattiṭṭam (10+2) vantapōtu oru pāṭattiṟku ivvāṟu tavaṟāka acciṭappeṟṟa cumār 40000 nūlkaḷ (nāṟpatāyiram) taḷḷappeṟṟu vēṟu putiya vakiyil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭateṉak kēḷvippaṭṭēṉ. (appōtu uyarnilip paḷḷikkuḻuvil nāṉum iṭam peṟṟiruntēṉ),

taṉiyār vacam oppuvittāl cilacamayaṅkaḷil tavaṟu uṇṭākiṟatu eṉpār kūṟṟu kātil viḻukiṉṟatu. vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ-allatu kiyūṭṭu atikamākat tarukiṉṟavarkaḷ allatu mēliṭattu ‘cipāricu’ peṟṟavarkaḷ pāṭanūlkaḷi -avi piḻipaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum veḷiyiṭa opputal taruvatum, attakiya nūlkaḷi appaṭiyē kiyūṭṭu mutaliyaṉa peṟṟu paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷil vikkappeṟuvatum uṇṭu eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāmum avartam kūṟṟiṉiyum nūṟṟukku nūṟupoy eṉṟu taḷḷavum muṭiyātu. palkalikaḻakap pāṭanūl kuḻuviṉar inta vakiyil ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa pēccu aṭikkaṭi kātil viḻukiṟatu. inta āṇṭu oru palkalikkaḻakattil oru tamiḻppakuti nīkkappeṟṟu vēṟu amikka ēṟpāṭu ceyyappeṟṟati nāḷitaḻkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. eṉavē kuṟi etilum illāmal illi. eṉiṉum palavaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟit terinteṭuttu nallaṉa ceytu vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭuvatu aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamiyākum-iṉṟiyamiyātatumākum.

perumpālum uyarnili, mēlnilippaḷḷippāṭaṅkaḷit tokukkum poṟuppiṉik kallūri āciriyarkaḷiṭam viṭukiṉṟatu. itu eṇṇippārkka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu. kallūriyil āciriyap payiṟci peṟāta āciriyarkaḷ koḷvōr koḷki aṟintu, iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷ aṟintu pāṭaṅkaḷi amikka iyalātu eṉpatu palar karuttu. mattiya paḷḷikaḷil 10,12 ākiya tērvu eḻutum vakuppukaḷ tavirttu muṟṟiya aṉittu vakuppukaḷukkum taṉiyār pāṭanūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa-taramāka uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē payilpavar ciṟakka varukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa niliyum uṇṭākiṟatu. iti māṟṟuvatil tavaṟu illi. aṟiviyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷi vēṇṭumāyiṉ attakiya uyarniliyil uḷḷavar iraṇṭoruvar tuṇikoṇṭu veḷiyiṭalām. āṉāl moḻippāṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi payiṟṟupavarkaḷil aṉupavam mutirntavarkaḷik koṇṭu tokukkappeṟiṉ payaṉ viḷiyum.

aṇmiyil mattiya aracāṅkam karumpalakit tiṭṭam eṉṟa oru tiṭṭattil ārampappaḷḷikaḷil payilum māṇavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil kōṭikkaṇakkil vārivaḻaṅkiyatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil antat tokiyiṉit takka vakiyil-akkuḻantikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil nūlkaḷit tērnteṭukkavilli eṉṟu cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭic cila itaḻkaḷ kaṇṭaṉam terivittaṉa. āṉāl ati maṟuttu aracāṅkamō anta nūlkaḷukku uriyavarkaḷō enta maṟuppum veḷiyiṭāta kāraṇattāl akkuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷ uṇmi eṉṟu koḷḷattāṉē vēṇṭi uḷḷatu. eṉavē ittakiya kuṟipāṭukaḷ illāvakiyil māṇavartam etirkāla vāḻkkikkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil nalla pāṭa nūlkaḷi veḷikkoṇara muyalavēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭukkoḷkiṟēṉ. ovvoru vakuppiṟkum amikkum pāṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum piṟavaṟṟip paṟṟiyum antantat tuṟikaḷit taṉiyākak kāṇumpōtu viḷakkalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

tāymoḻiyilēyē kalvi amiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu viṭutalikku muṉpē makkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntaṉar. irājāji mutalvarāka irunta kālattil ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum nalla tamiḻccoṟkaḷik kaṇṭu taṉittaṉi (ovvoru pāṭattiṟkum) akarāti nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil eḻutuvōrkku ūkkam aḷittār irājāji. ataṟkup piṟakum orumuṟi inta vakiyil ellāp pāṭaṅ kaḷukkum taṉittaṉi akarātikaḷ vantaṉa. mattiya aracum oru moḻikku oru kōṭi aḷavil avvam moḻiyil pāṭa

ka—2 nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭuvataṟkeṉa māṉiyamākat tantu ūkkiyatu. piṟa mānilaṅkaḷellām avaṟṟic celavu ceytu nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. cila mānilaṅkaḷ koṭutta māṉiyam pōtātu eṉṟu mēlum kēṭṭaṉa. āṉāl tamiḻakam taṉakkeṉa māṉiyamākat tantatil peruntokiyiṉit tirumpat tantatu eṉak kūṟakkēṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. ‘tamiḻ’ ‘tamiḻ’ eṉṟu mēṭiyil pēcuvatē aṉṟic ceyalil kāṭṭuvar ilarē eṉa varuntavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu iṉṟu (I.A.S.) ai.ē.es. mutaliya tērvukaḷil tamiḻnāṭu eṅkēyō piṉtaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉa araciyal talivarkaḷum āḷvōrum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru kālattil mutaliṭattil irunta tamiḻakam iṉṟu kaṭi iṭattil varakkāraṇm eṉṉa? pāṭaṅkaḷi avvam moḻiyil payilalām eṉṟa nili maṟṟa mānilaṅkaḷil iruppatōṭu ai.ē.es. tērviṉiyum avaravar tāymoḻiyil eḻutalām eṉṟa vitiyum uḷḷatu. vaṭanāṭṭavarum piṟarum intiyilum piṟa moḻikaḷilum eḻuti avvam moḻi aṟintavarālēyē tiruttappeṟṟu nalla iṭaṅkaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu ai.ē.es. eḻutukiṉṟavarkaḷ ettaṉi pēr tamiḻil eḻutukiṉṟaṉar? yār eṇṇip pārppavar?

kāl nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ ai.ē.es. tērviṟkut tamiḻil orutāḷ amikka virumpiyapōtu, nāṉum tiru te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār avarkaḷum pāṭat tiṭṭam vakuttu oru tāḷi eḻuta ēṟpāṭu ceytōm. avarkaḷ mutal iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum nāṉ piṉ iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum tērvāḷarāka iruntōm. mikak kuṟivākavē -oru cilarē eḻuta muṉvantaṉar. iṉṟum antat tāḷ irukkiṟatu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. (tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu-moḻi varalāṟu). ati eḻutupavarkaḷ uḷarō illiyō eṉpatu teriyātu.

eṉavē tamiḻaṉ pēcciṉi viṭuttut tāymoḻiyil kalviyik kaṟṟut teḷintālaṉṟi, pārata nāṭṭup piṟa pakutikaḷōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭa muṭiyātu. iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil māvaṭṭa āṭciyil pātikkumēl tamiḻ aṟiyātavarkaḷākavē uḷḷamikkum, kōṭṭiyil pala ai.ē.es. atikārikaḷ tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkum ituvē kāraṇam. tilli mattiya aracāṅkattilum irupattintu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ettuṟiyil ceṉṟālum tamiḻarip perumpālum kāṇamuṭiyum. avarkaḷum iṅkiruntu celvōri aṉpuṭaṉ ēṟṟu āvaṉa ceytu utaviṉar. iṉṟu aṅkēyum tamiḻaṉik kāṇpataritu. ēṉ inta avala nili? itit tirutta vēṇṭāmā?

nāṉ āṅkilattiyō intiyiyō vēṟṟu moḻiyiyō nam māṇavar kaṟkavēṇṭām eṉṟu collavilli. ‘tāyk kolic cālpuṭittu eṉpārum uṇṭu’ eṉṟu muṉṉip periyavarkaḷ kūṟiyatu pōṉṟu tāymoḻiyik koliceytuviṭṭu vēṟṟumoḻiyiṉi ‘ōmpu’tal tavaṟu eṉavē cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉēṉ. tamiḻōṭu ettaṉi moḻi vēṇṭumāṉālum kaṟkalām tamiḻaṉ attuṇi aṟivum āṟṟalum tiṟaṉum peṟṟavaṉ. iti yārum maṟukkamuṭiyātu. āṉāl tamiḻukkut tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaliṭam taravēṇṭum eṉpatē eṉ vēṇṭukōḷ! itu putitaṉṟu. ippaṭiyē pala mānilaṅkaḷil-ulakil pala nāṭukaḷ uḷḷa niliyiṉi aṟiyavēṇṭum.

tamiḻnāṭṭil orucila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ āṅkilam payiṟṟuvikka aṭikkaṭi vitikaḷ māṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. orumuṟi āṟām vakuppil āṅkilam toṭaṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum, oru muṟi mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkām vakuppil toṭaṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum aracu āṇiyiṭṭatāka aṟikiṟēṉ. antak kālattiltāṉ taṉiyār āṅkilap paḷḷikaḷ mūlik koṉṟākat tōṉṟa ārampittaṉa. antō āṅkilam illiyā? eṉṟu oṉṟum aṟiyāk kirāmavācikaḷ uḷpaṭa, āṅkilap paḷḷi eṅkē eṅkē eṉṟu tēṭi avaṟṟuḷ tam piḷḷiyic cērkkappāṭupaṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu kaṭṭuppāṭṭukku aṭaṅkā vakiyil atu peruki viṭṭatu. avaṟṟi muṟippaṭuttuvatum peṟṟōrkaḷit teḷivupaṭuttuvatum aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamiyākum.

tamiḻnāṭṭip poṟutta vakiyil 10ām vakuppu variyil palappala vakiyil paḷḷikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. aracāṅka icivu peṟāta kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikaḷ, ārampap paḷḷikaḷ āyirakkaṇakkil perukiyuḷḷaṉa. piṉ āṟu mutal pattu variyilum ettaṉiyō vēṟupāṭu! māṉiyam peṟum es.es.el.ci eṉum pattām vakuppu vari uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ, āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷ, kīḻtticip (Oriental) paḷḷikaḷ, mattiyap paḷḷikaḷ, tilli kiṟistava caṅkam naṭattum paḷḷikaḷ, meṭrikulēṣaṉ paḷḷikaḷ eṉap palavakiyil paḷḷikaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutalatu aracāṅka muḻu māṉiyam peṟṟu naṭipeṟuvatu. āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷilō pāṭattiṭṭam-moḻit tiṭṭam mikak kuṟivāka uḷḷatu. kīḻtticip paḷḷikaḷum avvāṟē. mattiyap paḷḷikaḷum meṭrikulēṣaṉ paḷḷikaḷum āṅkilattiyē payiṟṟu moḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mattiyap paḷḷikaḷil inti payiṟci moḻiyāka irukkalām. ippaṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭu iḷañciṟuvarkaḷ payila iṭarppaṭum kalvi nili tēvitāṉā? aṉṟu āṅkilēyaṉ āṇṭakālattil āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷ, ammarapiṉaric cārnta kuḻantikaḷukkāka ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkalām. iṉṟu anta marapiṉar tamiḻ marapiṉarōṭu oṉṟiviṭṭaṉarē! mēlum appaḷḷikaḷil 100kku 95kku mēl maṟṟavarkaḷ tāṉē payilkiṉṟaṉar. āciriyarkaḷum appaṭiyē. ciṟupāṉmi karutiyō vēṟu kāraṇattālō aṉṟu ārampittālum attakiya nili iṉṟu illiyē! ciṟupāṉmik kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ aṉittum anta niliyil tāṉē uḷḷaṉa. ciṟupāṉmi niliyaṅkaḷil 100kku 90kku mēl maṟṟavarkaḷtāṉē payilkiṉṟaṉar. kiṟittavap paḷḷikaḷilum ciṟupāṉmiyōr naṭattum piṟa paḷḷikaḷilum, aṭippaṭiyil aminta ciṟupāṉmiyōr paḷḷikaḷilum payilvōruḷ 100kku 90kku mēl avvac ciṟupāṉmiyic cārātavarkaḷtāmē payilkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟic ciṟupāṉmiyōr kalviccālikaḷ eṉak koṇṭu taṉic calukikaḷ aḷippatu eppaṭip poruntum? avarkaḷē perumpāṉmiyirākavō muṟṟumākavō payiṉṟāl taṉiccalukikaḷ tēviyē! araciyal cācaṉattil itaṟku viḷakkam tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.

eṅkō ceṉṟu viṭṭēṉ. maṉṉikkavum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil ivvāṟu palavakik kalvikaḷ toṭakkaniliyil iruppatu tēvitāṉā! āṅkilap payiṟṟumoḻip paḷḷi kaḷil payilum māṇavar tāmē uyarntavar eṉṟu karutit taṉic cātiyāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷa vēṟupāṭu pōtātu eṉṟu intap putiya vēṟupāṭṭiṉi aracāṅkamē uṇṭākka vēṇṭumā? kalvittuṟi nirvākikaḷum amiccakamum karuttirutti uṭaṉ itaṟku maruntu kāṇa vēṇṭum. kalviyi mānila elliyilē koḷḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, mattiya aracuṭaṉ iṇittal tavaṟākum. iti uṇarum nāḷē nāṭṭil uṇmiyāṉa kalvi malarum nāḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭu aracāṅkam uṭaṉ tīviramākac cintaṉi ceytu toṭakkak kalvi niliyil (10m vakuppu vari) ittaṉi vēṟupāṭukaḷ illiyākac ceyyavēṇṭum. iḷam piñcu uḷḷaṅkaḷil kaṟkumpōtē māṟṟu eṇṇaṅkaḷi-vēṟupāṭṭu uṇarvukaḷi vaḷarkkum kalvi muṟi kaḷiyappaṭal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl nakattāl kiḷḷuvati viṭṭup piṉ kōṭari koṇṭu veṭṭa vēṇṭiya nili uṇṭākum. inta aṭippaṭi māṟṟam ceyyātu, ettaṉi orumippāṭu mānāṭukaḷ kūṭṭiṉālum vāṉoliyilum tolikkāṭciyilum varici variciyāka nikaḻccikaḷ amittālum payaṉ viḷiyātu. ārampak kalvi paṟṟiyum piṟa nilikaḷip paṟṟiyum uyarnilik kalvi nili mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum avvat talippukaḷil piṟaku ārāyalām.

nam nāṭṭil naṉku payiṉṟu ciṟakkap paṭṭam peṟupavar nam nāṭṭil taṅkātu piṟa nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟuviṭukiṉṟārkaḷē eṉṟu peruntalivarkaḷ uḷpaṭap palar varuntukiṉṟaṉar. aṇmiyil nam tamiḻaka mutalvar kūṭa attakiya karuttiṉi ōriṭattil pēciyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. ataṟku aṭippaṭik kāraṇatti ārāyavēṇṭum. nāṉ 1985il mēli nāṭukaḷukkum amerikkāvukkum ceṉṟa kāli aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷi itupaṟṟik kēṭṭēṉ. pala nalla maruttuvarkaḷ (Doctors) poṟiyiyalāḷarkaḷ (Engineers)-piṟa tuṟikaḷil vallunarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭic cērntavarkaḷ-aṅkaṅkē ciṟakkac ceyal purintu avvan nāṭṭi vaḷappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ovvoruvariyum nāṉ ‘tāynāṭṭi viṭṭu ēṉ vantīrkaḷ?’ eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. avarkaḷ aṉivarum kūṟiya patilkaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya orē muṭiviṉit taruvatākavē uḷḷaṉa. ‘nāṅkaḷ iṅkē niṟiyavē campātikkiṟōm. āṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭi viṭṭu vara maṉamillitāṉ. iṅkē campātippatil pattil oru pākam aṅkē campātittālum pōtum; maṉa niṟivōṭu vāḻvōm. iṅkē aṟivukku, tiṟaṉukku, āṟṟalukku, ākka vaḻikku uḻikkum uḻippukku mutaliṭam tarukiṟārkaḷ. aṅkēyō-tamiḻ nāṭṭil vēṇṭiyavarkaḷukku, kiyūṭṭu tarupavarkaḷukku taṉ contakkāraṉukku-jātikkāraṉukkut tāṉē mutaliṭam. avarkaḷukkē patavi uyarvu, piṟa ellāc calukikaḷum tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nintu pulampukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tiṟaṉāl aṅkē ettakiya aṟputakaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ ellām-poṟiyāḷarkaḷ-maruttuvarkaḷ-nōpal paricu peṟṟavarkaḷ-pērāciriyarkaḷ-piṟa tuṟi vallunarkaḷ-iṅkē tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntāl nāṭu evvaḷavu muṉṉēṟum eṉa eṇṇi nāṉ varuntiya nāṭkaḷ pala. eṉ nūlil (ēḻu nāṭukaḷil eḻupatu nāṭkaḷ) itu paṟṟic ciṟitu kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. iṅkum palar uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ kumuṟalōṭu vēli ceyvatiyum aṟivēṉ. ataṉāl pala tuṟikaḷil vēlikaḷ mantamāvatōṭu muṟiyaṟṟatākavum amikiṉṟaṉa. kalvit tuṟiyilēyē muṟiyāka mēlē varavēṇṭiya oruvarit taḷḷi vēṟoruvari uyarttiyati avar collic collik kaṇṇīr viṭukiṟār eṉak kēḷvippaṭukiṟēṉ. inta nili, nāṭṭil nīṅkiṉāl oḻiya, āyiram kōṭi kōṭiyāka nām kalvikkuc celaviṭum toki yārukkō tāṉ payaṉpaṭuvakiyil amiyum, iti nam nāṭṭu aracu naṉku eṇṇi, takkatiṉṉa takātaṉa iṉṉa eṉṟu āyntu ceyalpaṭal naṉṟu.

nāṟpattintāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kalvit tuṟi māvaṭṭak kaḻakattiṉkīḻ naṉku ceyalpaṭṭu vantatu. ‘District Board’ eṉṟum ‘Taluk Board’ eṉṟum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭaṅkaḷtoṟum āṭcip poṟuppi ēṟṟuc ceyalpurintaṉa. kalvi, cukātāram, cāli pōṉṟa mukkiya tuṟikaḷ akkaḻakattiṉ vacam iruntaṉa. ataṉ uṟuppiṉar ovvoruvarum nāṟpatu allatu aimpatu kirāmattiṟku eṉat tērnteṭukkappaṭuvar. ovvoruvarum ciṟu aḷavil uḷḷa tam elliyiṉi aṭikkaṭi pārviyiṭṭu, makkaḷ tēvi aṟintu tiṅkaḷukku oru muṟi naṭakkum kaḻakak kūṭṭattil attēvikaḷi vaṟpuṟutti makkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟic ceytuvantaṉar. atikāram ivvāṟu paravalāka iruntatōṭu, uṟuppiṉarum tam elliyic cārntavarāka iruntatāl makkaḷum avarkaḷiṭam virumpic ceṉṟu tattam kuṟikaḷi muṟiyiṭuvar. maṟṟum ūrtoṟum uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷ ciṟakkap paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉarā eṉa mēṟpārvi pārkkavum vacatiyāka iruntatu. vittiya vacatikaḷum cāli vacatikaḷum avvāṟē pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. kalvittuṟi neṟiyil māvaṭṭak kalvi aluvalar, vaṭṭa āyvāḷarkaḷiṉ nērmukap pārviyōṭu, ammāvaṭṭak kaḻaka uṟuppiṉar aṭikkaṭi paḷḷikaḷip pārviyiṭṭu vantamiyiṉ paḷḷikaḷ muṟiyāka naṭipeṟṟaṉa eṉalām. kallūri tavirttu maṟṟiya ellāp paḷḷikaḷum ivarkaḷ mēṟpārviyilēyē iruntaṉa.

iṉṟō inta nili illi. pañcāyattu oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyaraḷavil iruppiṉum ataṉ elli virintuḷḷamiyiṉ taṉi uṟuppiṉar nērmukap pārvi arukiviṭṭatu. muṉ iruntami pōṉṟu oṉṟiyattukkeṉa, kalviyik kavaṉittup pōṟṟa, taṉik kalvi aluvalakamum kiṭiyātu. araciyal cūḻalāl aṭikkaṭi inta oṉṟiyaṅkaḷum kalikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ūrtōṟum kalviyil muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇa muṭiyavilli eṅkiruntō āyvāḷar-muṉ aṟivippuṭaṉ varumpōtu eppaṭiyō vakuppukaḷic carikaṭṭik kāṭṭiviṭṭup pala nāḷ mūṭik kiṭakkum paḷḷikaḷ pala uḷa. taṉiyāciriyar paḷḷikaḷip paṟṟiyō colla vēṇṭiyatilli. takka nallāciriyarkaḷum taṟkālattil kiṭippataritu. eṉavē kirāmap paḷḷikaḷil nalla muṟiyil kalvi amiya vēṇṭumāyiṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiya nallavar kuḻukkaḷ tēvi. iṅkuḷḷa piṟa nilikaḷippaṟṟip piṉpu taṉiyākak kāṇalām.

vayatu vantōrukku eṉak kalvi kaṟpikkum muṟi nāṭṭil pōṟṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. ataṟkeṉak kōṭik kaṇakkil mattiya aracum mānila aracum pōṭṭiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu celavu ceykiṉṟaṉa. pala taṉiyār niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷumkūṭa ōraḷavu utavukiṉṟaṉa. paḷḷi, kallūri māṇavarkaḷ itil īṭupaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum celavukku ēṟṟa payaṉ viḷivatilli. ēṭṭuk kaṇakkiṉi nāṭṭu nikaḻcciyōṭu oppiṭṭup pārppiṉ uṇmi viḷaṅkum. taṟpōtu putiya vēkattil māvaṭṭantōṟum inta muyaṟci ceyalākkappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘eḻuttaṟiyār inta māvaṭṭattil illiyākac ceyvōm’ eṉṟu ovvoru māvaṭṭa āṭcit talivarum avaric cārntōrkaḷum muyalkiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum makkaḷ ottuḻipput tēvi. atip poṟuttiruntutāṉ kāṇa vēṇṭum. ellārum kaṟṟōrē eṉṟa nili nāṭṭil malariṉ antanāḷ naṉṉāḷākum eṉpatu uṇmi! āṉāl annāḷ eṉṟu varumō!

ilavacak kalvi paṟṟiyum caṟṟu eṇṇip pārkkat tōṉṟukiṟatu. muṉpellām ārampappaḷḷi vakuppukaḷ aintām vakuppuvari eṅkum ilavacamākavē iyaṅkiṉa. orucila taṉiyār-kiṟittavar paḷḷikaḷ pōṉṟavi mikak kuṟinta aḷavil campaḷam peṟṟaṉa. paḷḷiyil mattiya vakuppukaḷilum (6, 7, 8) mēl vakuppukaḷilum (9, 10, 11) mikak kuṟivākavē campaḷam peṟṟaṉar. kallūrikaḷilum appaṭiyē. iṉṟu mēnilippaḷḷi variyil kalvi ilavacamākkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. ‘itu tēvitāṉā’ eṉṟa viṉā palar uḷḷattil eḻukiṉṟatu. ēḻikaḷukku utava aṉṟum ceṉṉik kalvi vitikaḷil 72il taṉiviti iruntatu. akkuṟinta campaḷattiyum avarkaḷ kaṭṭavēṇṭiyatilli. appōtu irunta taram iṉṟu kalviyil illi. pala peṟṟōr paṇam celavilliyātalāl piḷḷikaḷ paṭippip paṟṟik kavalippaṭuvatilli. nāṭeṅkum ilavacap paḷḷikaḷ irukkumpōtu, nūṟum irunūṟum mātaccampaḷamum peṟṟu, cērkkumpōtu cila āyiraṅkaḷum naṉkoṭi vāṅkum piṟa paḷḷikaḷ vaḷarvataṟkuriya kāraṇatti eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum. pala ēḻikaḷum kūṭa, piṟa celavukaḷik kuṟittukkoṇṭu campaḷam kaṭṭum paḷḷikaḷukkē piḷḷikaḷi aṉuppak kāṇkiṟōm. ‘eṉavē ilavacak kalvi eṉil taram kuṟintatu’ eṉṟu makkaḷ niṉikkum aḷavukkut taṉiyār māṉiyam peṟum paḷḷikaḷum aracāṅkap paḷḷikaḷum ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru vakuppiṟku 40 allatu 45 eṉpatupōka 75 allatu 80 vari māṇavari aṭittuvikkum avala niliyiṉic cila paḷḷikaḷil kāṇkiṟōm. cūṉ mātam toṭaṅkiya paḷḷikku aṭutta āṇṭu pipravariyil tēviyāṉa āciriyari aṉuppukiṉṟa aracāṅka vitimuṟikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. āciriyarkaḷum, muṉṉiviṭap pala maṭaṅku-eṇṇip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷaviṟku-campaḷam vāṅkukiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum piḷḷikaḷ kalviyil pōtiya karuttiṉic celuttavilli eṉap peṟṟōrkaḷum, talivarkaḷum, āḷunar, amiccar pōṉṟārkaḷum collukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ēḻikaḷukku eṉat taṉic caluki aḷittuc campaḷam illiyākki, iṉṟiya nilikkut tēviyāṉa campaḷatti ōrām vakuppu mutal variyaṟuttu vacūl ceytāl paṇam kaṭṭum peṟṟōrkkum akkaṟi uṇṭākum. araciṉ kaṇkāṇippum atikamākkappeṟal vēṇṭum. toṭakkak kalviyum aṭutta uyarnilik kalviyum taram uyarntu takka vakiyil māṇavarukku aḷikkappeṟṟāltāṉ piṉ kallūrik kalviyil teḷivum taramum kāṇamuṭiyum. eṉavē atikak kaṇkāṇippum kaṇṭippum ārampa uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷukkut tēviyāṉavi. ilavacak kalviyuṭaṉ araciyalum iṇikkap peṟṟiruppatāl eṉ karuttiṉi ēṟṟukkoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu teriyum. eṉiṉum uḷḷa niliyiṉit teḷḷatteḷiyak kāṭṭavē itik kuṟittēṉ.

ippaṭiyē ilavaca pas pās vaḻaṅkalum. itu vēṇṭumeṉṟu yārum kēṭkavilli. perumpālum paḷḷip piḷḷikaḷ tattam vāḻiṭaṅkaḷukku arukil uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷilēyē iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉar. campaḷam vāṅkum paḷḷikaḷil perumpālaṉa untuvaṇṭikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē inta nili tēviyaṟṟatu. itaṉāl tēvi illātavarkaḷkūṭa iraṇṭoru niṟuttumiṭamāyiṉum ataṉ muṟṟiya elli variyil ilavacaccīṭṭu vāṅki untu vaṇṭikaḷil nericali uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum itit tayārippataṟkum takavalkaḷ cērttu aṉuppuvataṟkum pōkkuvarattu aluvalakamum ovvoru paḷḷiyum peṟum allalum avatiyum eḻuta muṭiyā! pala paḷḷikaḷil itaṉāl muṟiyāṉa vēliyum taṭippaṭukiṟatu. peṟṟōrkaḷum māṇavarkaḷum uṭaṉ vāṅkit taravilliyē eṉa vātiṭum niliyum uṇṭākiṟatu. ippaṭit tēviyaṟṟa vakiyil-araciyal piṉṉaṇiyil amiyum cila ceyalkaḷip paṟṟi atikam eḻutāmal iruppatē nalameṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

ituvari kūṟiyavāṟṟāl tamiḻakak kalviyiṉ muntiya ēṟṟam, iṭikkālap piṉṉaṭivu, iṉṟiya kuṟikaḷ, paḷḷikaḷiṉ vakikaḷ, piṟanāṭṭu niliyil kalvi amippu mutaliyavaṟṟi ōraḷavu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. nīkka vēṇṭiyavi paṟṟiyum ākka vēṇṭiyavi paṟṟiyum camutāya muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum tamiḻakam muṉpōṉṟu muṉ niṉṟu uyarnili peṟutaṟkum vaḻittuṟikaḷi ōraḷavu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iṉi ovvoru tuṟiyiṉiyum taṉittaṉiyē koṇṭu atatil uḷḷa nalakkēṭukaḷiyum tēviyāṉa tiruttaṅkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum paṟṟi ārāyalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

mēlum āciriyar nili, māṇavar, peṟṟōr nili, nāṭṭu naṭappu, aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭami pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum uṭaṉ eṇṇip pārttalum ēṟpuṭittākum eṉa niṉikkiṉṟēṉ. toṭarntu nāṭṭuk kalvi nalam peṟṟu, ulaka araṅkil nam pāratamum ciṟappākat tamiḻakamum uyarveyti ulakōr pōṟṟum nili eytavēṇṭum eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉēyē iṉi varuvaṉavaṟṟi eḻuta niṉikkiṟēṉ. oruvariyum kuṟicolluvataṟkeṉṟō kuṟṟam cāṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟō vēṟu paḻipaṭarniliyilō nāṉ ivaṟṟi eḻutavilli. ‘oṉṟē ceyyavum vēṇṭum, oṉṟum naṉṟē ceyyavum vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyil nāṭṭu nalaṉ-ataṉ kaṇṇākiya kalvi-ati makkaḷukku aḷikkum marapum muṟiyum-ivi paṟṟiyē eṉ eṇṇamum eḻuttum paṟṟip paṭarum eṉakkūṟi mēlē cellukiṉṟēṉ.

nam nāṭṭuk kalvi vaḷarāmikku intiya aracāṅkam amitta kalvi āyvu paṟṟiyum tirutta vēṇṭiya muṟi paṟṟiyum viḷakka amitta kuḻuviṉ amippāḷar tiru. irāmamūrtti avartam muṉṉuriyil kūṟiyavaṟṟi appaṭiyē uṅkaḷ muṉvikkiṟēṉ.

One fundamental reason for failure has been that while we go on making radical protestations, our education to this day continues to be governed by the same assumptions, goals and values that governed it in the days of the British Raj. The British believed in the ‘downward filtration theory’ under which education and culture would inevitably flow from the classes to the masses. They kept the common people away from education. and education away from life. But things have not much changed since they left. Even today the principal beneficiaries of our education are the upper and middle classes. To them also we give a wrong education. Our formal system remins confined to the four walls of a school or college. It is tied down to text books and examinations. Even then the books are unreadable and the examinations totally unreliable, The courses of study are so framed that the students are not equipped with any productive skills. Whatever education they receive cuts them off from their natural and social environment. They become aliens to their own community. They lose faith in life itself what Jayaprakashji wrote in 1978 still holds true. According to him ‘it also converts them into a parasitic class which perpetuates and even intensifies the poverty of the masses. The system has failed to promote individual growth. It also becomes more of a hindrance than a help to bring about an egalitarian transformation’. If this be true, can we say that we have basically departed from the Maculay tradition? And, if this is what our education has done to us, one may well ask, is not no education better than bad education?



One may admit—that for this situation education alone is not responsible, During the last forty-three years we have pursued a model of economic development that has led to the creation of two Indias—one of the rich, the other of the poor. A new privileged class has come into being. It holds monopoly over political and economic power and sources. of wealth. It controls culture and education. It is firmly established every where. It is this class whose interests our education is made to serve. The result is that as in economy so in education, two parallel systems have come into being– one for the rich, the other for the poor. No wonder, a divided education finds itself totally devitalised, and incapable of meeting the challenges of independent India's national life. To the rise and growth of this class, holding sway over the whole range of national affairs, can be traced most of the ills we are faced with— the erosion of social and moral values, weakening of democracy, the partisan character of our development, corruption and a number of other elitist aberrations. It is responsible for the impoverishment of the nation's very soul. It is, therefore time the nation, most of all education, took serious note of this phenomenon, and guarded aginst further damage to national life.

Report of the committee for Review of

National Policy of Education—1986

— Pages - V & VI



uyarntōr karuttaṟintu uṟṟatic ceyyiṉ uyti uṇṭu. takkavar-aracāṅka neṟiyāḷar uṭaṉ āmvu ceyvārkaḷā



veḷḷattāl pōkātu ventaṇalāl vēkātu vēntarālum





koḷḷattāṉ muṭiyātu koṭuttālum niṟivoḻiyak kuṟipaṭātu





kaḷḷarkkō mikaaritu kāvalō mikeḷitu kalvi eṉṉum





uḷḷattē poruḷirukka ulakelām poruḷtēṭi uḻalvatēṉō!





﻿2. palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ

nāṭṭiṉ uyarkalviyiṉ ceḻippiyum tarattiyum avaṟṟiṉvaḻi teḷivākum paṇpāṭu, aṟiviyal teḷivu, ākkaneṟi ākiyavaṟṟiyum ulakukku uṇarttuvaṉa avvannāṭṭup palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷē. ataṉālēyē attakiya palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷi nāṭi namnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ palar veḷināṭukaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. intiyāvi, tamiḻnāṭṭi avvāṟu palar nāṭivantakālamum oṉṟu iruntatu. palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ oḷi viḷakkaṅkaḷāy ulaka aṟiñarkaḷit tammiṭam īrkkum tiṟaṉ uṭiyaṉavāy-eṉṟeṉṟum ulakil vāḻum camutāya aṟiviyal, vāḻviyal nuṭpaṅkaḷi. āyntu kaṇṭu uṇarttuvaṉavāy amiyavēṇṭum. kalviyeṉum:māḷikiyil uyarnta mēlmāṭiyāka niṉṟu, paṭippaṭiyāka ēṟivarum aṟiñari-cāṉṟōri muḻumipeṟac ceyvaṉa ivi. kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi nērmi uṭiyaṉavāy, kālam kaṭavāk kaṭappāṭṭil niṉṟu, nērmiyil nilittuniṉṟu, vāḻum camutāyattukkum varuṅkālac camutāyattukkum vaḻikāṭṭum tiṟaṉum ceḻippum ceyalpāṭum tiṇmiyum uṭiyaṉavāka amiyavēṇṭum. vaḷḷuvar ‘kalvi’ paṟṟi vakutta koḷkikku nilikkaḷaṉāy, ‘tām peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivviyakam’ eṉṟa uṇarvuṭiyōri ulakukkut takkavarākki āṇṭutōṟum aḷittu utavuvaṉavē ivi. iṅkē kāḻppukkum kacappukkum vēṟṟumikkum vēṟupāṭṭukkum iṭamilli. vañcakattukkum vaṉkaṇmikkum vaḻakkukkum vātattukkum iṭam illi. ām! ivviṭam tulākkōl pōṉṟu nērmi vaḻaṅkum kaṭavuḷ cannitāṉam pōṉṟa oṉṟākum. mēṉilippaḷḷiyiṉ kalvi ulakap potuvāḻvukku vēṇṭiya vāḻkkik kalviyik kaṟṟu, piṉ vāḻvukku vakitēṭa amiyum niliyil, celuttum vakiyil amivatākum. āṉāl palkalikkaḻakak kalviyō ataṉ peyarukku ēṟṟapaṭi ulakaḷāviyatāy (Universaly) ēṉ paranta aṇṭa kōḷa elli variyil pārttu āyntu nalaṉ kāṇpatāy taṉakku maṭṭumaṉṟi, taṉ kālattukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, eṉṟeṉṟum vāḻum maṉita camutāyattukkum uyiriṉattukkum uṟutuṇiyāy amivatākum.mēlum payilpavarukku immikku maṭṭumaṉṟi vaḷḷuvar kūṟiya eḻukiṉṟa piṟavikaḷtōṟum avaṉukku vaḻikāṭṭi avaṉi naṭatticcelvatu. palkalikkaḻakam eṉṟa collē poruḷ potintatākum. ‘University’ eṉṟa āṅkilac collukku mūlakkaru ‘Universe’ eṉpatākum. ām! paranta aṇṭakōḷa amippiṉ toṭarpuṭiyatāy- ‘ellāp poruḷum itaṉpāl uḷa’ eṉa aṉittiyum aṭakkiyatāy-eṉṟeṉṟum paranta ulakil maṭṭumiṉṟi virinta aṇṭakōḷattil vāḻnta-vāḻum-vāḻa irukkum uyirttaṉmikaḷi uṇarntu, avaṟṟiṉ virivākkam-avi koṇṭa toṭarpu-avaṟṟōṭu aminta pañcapūta oṭukkam eṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum koṇṭatāy-atil payilvōr teḷinta uḷḷamum teḷḷiya aṟivum kūrtta matiyum nalam koñcum neñcamum uṭiyavarkaḷāy, evvuyiriyum ottunōkkum iyalpiṉarāy irukkavēṇṭum. māntarkkuk kaṇ eṉa aminta ikkalvi ittakiya paranta -virinta-teḷinta-nuṇṇiya-āynta taṉmiyil uḷḷatāka amiyavēṇṭum. āṉāl iṉṟiya palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ-nam tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ intap peruniliyil uḷḷaṉavā? eṇṇippāruṅkaḷ!

ulakeṅkum cuṟṟi mēlināṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum kīḻināṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum kaṇṭavaṉ nāṉ. avaṟṟōṭu nam nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷi oppunōkkiṉ nām eṅkō piṉniliyil irukkiṉṟōm eṉpati aṟiyamuṭiyum. namnāṭṭu aṟiviyal nuṇukkaṅkaḷi viḷakkum paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku mēli nāṭṭup palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ viḷakkam tarukiṉṟaṉa; pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. pārāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avipaṟṟi, mēlum mēlum ārāykiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl iṅkō avaṟṟip paṭikka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ kūṭap paṭippatilli. ataṉ payaṉ eṉṉa eṉṟu eṇṇippārppatilli. nam nāṭṭu aṟiviyal, paṇpāṭu, kali, nalattuṟi pōṉṟa palavaṟṟi mēlināṭṭup palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ ārāyum pōtu, nām avaṟṟip paṭikkavum ceyyātu pāḻāka nāṭkaḷik kaḻikkiṉṟōm. ivaṟṟit teḷivupaṭutti ulakukku uṇarttavēṇṭiya-vaḻaṅkavēṇṭiya palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ eti etiyō eṭuttu ārāykiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻamiyāṉa palkalikkaḻkattiṉ āyvuppaṭṭaṅkaḷukku eḻutappeṟṟa kaṭṭurikaḷi oru kaṇṇōṭṭam koṇṭu niṉṟāl avaṟṟuḷ ettaṉi camutāyattukkup payaṉpaṭuvaṉa eṉa eṇṇattōṉṟum. tamiḻttuṟi oṉṟiṉi maṭṭum nāṉ cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉēṉ. piṟa tuṟikaḷip paṟṟi avvat tuṟiyiṉar naṉku aṟivar. āṉāl atē vēḷiyil varalāṟṟup piṉṉaṇiyil-vāḻvoṭu toṭarpuṭiya aṟiviyal ākka amippil nam ilakkiyaṅkaḷ evvevvāṟu vaḷampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpatupaṟṟi amerikkap palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ ārāykiṉṟaṉa. avvannāṭṭu aṟiviyal ilakkiyaneṟi, camutāyaneṟi, camayaneṟi pōṉṟavaṟṟōṭu, piṟanāṭṭu nallaṉavaṟṟi nāṭi nalam kāṇum appalkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷip pārāṭṭak kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. ceṉṟiṭuvīr eṭṭuttikkum kaliccelvaṅkaḷ yāvum koṇarntiṅku cērppīr eṉṟu pāratiyiṉ pāṭalip pāṭi avarukku nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāk koṇṭāṭi amintuviṭukiṟōmē aṉṟi avar colvaḻiyē ceyalāṟṟa nam palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ muyalkiṉṟaṉavā? nām avar



ka–3 peyarimaṭṭum vittu tirupti aṭikiṟōm. avvaḷavē!

tamiḻnāṭṭu mikap paḻiya palkalikkaḻakam ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakantāṉ. atu 1857ām āṇṭu cepṭampar aintām nāḷ tōṉṟiyatu. ataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpāka atē āṇṭil pampāy, kalkattā palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉa aṟikiṟōm. ittakiya paḻamperum palkalikkaḻakam paranta elliyiṉik kaṭṭi āṇṭatu. kēraḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, āntirap pakutikaḷiyum aitarāpāt elliyiṉiyum aṉṟu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam koṇṭiruntatu. iṉṟu oricāvuṭaṉ irukkum ‘kañcam toṭaṅkik kumari variyilum mēl kīḻ kaṭal elliyilum itu āṭci celuttiyatu. ettaṉiyō vallavarkaḷ. nallavarkaḷ itil iruntu nāṭṭu mēṉilik kalviyiṉi naṉku vaḷarttu vantaṉar. iṉṟu moḻi vāri mānilaṅkaḷ pirittapiṉ, piṟamoḻip pakutikaḷ nīṅkiyatōṭu aṉṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭilum mēlum patiṉmūṉṟu palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, itaṉ elliyiṉi mikac curuṅka vittuviṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum aṉṟu itaṉ āṇiyiṉkīḻ irunta kallūrikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyiṉik kāṭṭilum iṉṟu eṇṇikki atikamāka uḷḷatu. maruttuvam, vivacāyam, poṟiyiyal, kālnaṭi pōṉṟa vakikkoru palkalikkaḻakam amintu i ṉ aṭippaṭip pakutikaḷiyum curuṅkac ceytuviṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻakattil taṉippaṭa aṇṇāmali, aḻakappar, aviṉāciliṅkam pōṉṟōruṭiya palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ. ampāttuṟi kānti kirāmam palkalikkaḻakam ākiyaviyum taṉi āṇi celuttukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻukkum makaḷirukkum eṉṟum taṉip palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ākavē pāṭa aḷavilum parappaḷavilum elli curuṅkiyatākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakam niṉṟuviṭṭatu. taṉippaṭṭa palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ tavirttu, kōvi, tirucci, maturi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷavi tammuṭaṉ pala kallūrikaḷi iṇittut tērvu naṭattukiṉṟaṉa. ceṉṉi nakarilēyē iṉṟu palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum avi taṉippaṭṭavi. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakam maṭṭumē kallūrikaḷi iṇittut tērvu naṭattukiṉṟatu. iṅkē ellāp palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum eṇṇip pārttu eḻuta iṭamilli. muṟṟum eṉṉāl aṟintu koḷḷavum muṭiyartu. eṉavē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakatti maṭṭum eṭuttuk kāṇa niṉikkiṉṟēṉ. itu aṟivu vaḷam peṟṟavaṉākki eṉṉi vaḷarttup peyariṭṭu ulakil ulavaviṭṭa palkalik kaḻakam allavā! itu paṟṟi nāṉ kāṭṭum nallaviyō allaviyō piṟavaṟṟiṟkum poruntum eṉṟum kūṟamāṭṭēṉ.

nāṉ muṉpē cuṭṭiyapaṭi mēli nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ ceyaltiṟaṉi oppu nōkkumpōtu nām eṅkō piṉṉiliyiltāṉ uḷḷōm. aṅkellām payiṟṟu muṟiyum, pāṭa amippum, tērvu muṟiyum, piṟa ceyalmuṟikaḷum namakku vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāka uḷḷaṉa. ōr āciriyar-attuṟiyil teḷinta aṟivum tiṟaṉum uṭiyavar, tam kīḻ orucila māṇavariyē terinteṭuttuk koṇṭu, ētēṉum oru pāṭattil taṉit tiṟami kāṭṭum vakiyil payiṟṟuvittu, pāṭam pōṟṟi, tēviyāṉapōtu vakuppukaḷamittu-nalla nūl niliyaṅkaḷil āyvu ceyyap paṇittu, eṭutta poruḷi naṉku viḷakki, ulakukku uṇarttum vakiyil tayārākkip paṭṭamaḷikka vāyppu aḷikkum muṟi uṇṭu. iṅkō yārō eṉṟō variyaṟutta pāṭaṅkaḷik kūṭik kūṭṭamāka iruntu, ētō pēci eppaṭiyō eḻutip paṭṭam peṟum niliyiṉik kāṇkiṟōm. vakuppiṟku varāmalēyē māṇavar vantatākak kāṭṭiyum, āciriyar kallūrikku vārāmalēyē vantatākak kāṭṭiyum māṇavarukkum tamakkum tammi aṟiyāmalēyē piḻi purivōr cilar uḷḷaṉar. eṉṟu kūṟak kēṭkiṟōm. āciriyarkaḷ toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷ amittu, payaṉuḷḷa muṟiyaṉṟi māṟupaṭuvatip palar cuṭṭik kāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. palkalikkaḻakattilum ataṉ aṅkaṅkaḷākiya kallūrikaḷilum iṉṟiya nili varuntattakkatāka uḷḷatu. etaṟkeṭuttālum vēli niṟuttamum uṇṇāviratamum mēṟkoḷḷum muṟi ittūya teyvattalaṅkaḷil naṭipeṟuvatu varuntattakkatu. attakiya avalanilikku avarkaḷit taḷḷāta vakiyil āṭciyāḷarum ceyalpaṭavēṇṭum.

oru pāṭattukku nāṉku allatu mūṉṟu āciriyar eṉap pāṭam naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl māṇavar tam āṟṟalum uṇarvum citaṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉa: paṇṭik kālattiya kurukulam iṉṟēṉum, oru pāṭattiṉi-allatu tērvukku oru tāḷukkuriya pāṭattiṉi orē āciriyar naṭattiṉ nalla payaṉ viḷiyum. āṉāl pāṭattiṭṭamō payiṟṟu muṟiyō avvāṟu amivatilli. āciriyarkaḷum, muṉṉarē nāṉ kāṭṭiyapaṭi entap payiṟciyum peṟāmal tiṭīreṉak kaṟpikka vantu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. āciriyarkaḷukkuk kuṟintatu iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷāvatu payiṟci aḷikka aracāṅkam ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum. appaṭic ceyyum poḻutu, piṟa taṉic ciṟappuk kallūrikaḷukku amikkum vakiyil, kuṟintatu nūṟṟukku eḻupatu eṇṇāvatu peṟṟavarkaḷiyē tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.* (cātāraṇ paṭṭa vakuppil ‘pi.pi.e.’ eṉṟa vakuppiṟkum nūṟṟukku aṟupatukkuk kuṟiyāta matippeṇ vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu) avarkaḷ ētēṉum oru pāṭattiṉit tiṟampaṭa naṭattum vakiyil ēṟṟa payiṟci aḷikkap peṟal vēṇṭum. inta vakiyil nallāciriyarkaḷ amivārkaḷāyiṉ-avarkaḷum taṅkaḷ kaṭami uṇarntu tiṟampaṭac ceyalāṟṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ nalla māṇavamaṇikaḷ nāṭṭukkum ulakukkum kiṭikkumē!

ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam oru kālattil mikac ciṟantatākap pōṟṟap peṟṟu, ulaka araṅkil ataṉ māṇavarkaḷukku uriyamatippum naliṭaṅkaḷum tarappeṟṟiṉa. aṉittintiyat tērvukaḷilum mutaliṭam peṟṟut tikaḻntatu. iṉṟu palkalikkaḻakam taṉṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tāḻntamiyāṉum kallūrikaḷum veṟum vāṇipa niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷāka māṟivarukiṉṟamiyāṉum tarattiṉik kāṇa muṭivatilli. matippeṇ aṭippaṭiyil cērppaṉa viṭuttu paṇatteṇṇik kaṇakkiṭṭuc cērkkum kallūrikaḷē atikamākiviṭṭaṉa. maruttuvak kallūrikku aintulaṭcam āṟulaṭcam variyilum poṟiyiyal kallūrikku iraṇṭu laṭcam variyilum pēraṅkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. cuyanitik kallūri allatu māṉiyam peṟāta kallūrikaḷ palavum peruntoki peṟuvatu orupuṟam irukka, ellā utavikaḷiyum aracāṅkattiṭam peṟum kallūrikaḷum nāṟpatu, aimpatu, āyiram variyil pāṭattukku ēṟpa vāṅkukiṉṟaṉavē! ivaṟṟit taṭukka yār niṉikkiṟārkaḷ? cuyanitik kallūrikaḷ vacūlikkum nitikku ucca varampu tēvi eṉṟu kalvi māṉiyak kōrikkiyiṉpōtu caṭṭa maṉṟattil oruvar cuṭṭi irukkiṟār. (tiṉamaṇi15-9-91-pak 60 eṉavē itu nāṭaṟinta oṉṟāṉatōṭu, aracum aṟinta oṉṟāki viṭṭatallavā! palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟik kaṇṭippataṟkup patilāka ūkkuvikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa pēccē eṅkum aṭipaṭukiṟatu. kallūrikaḷil tērvu naṭattum muṟiyilum cīrkēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpar.

palkalik kaḻakac ceyaṟpāṭukaḷilum mikunta kuṟipāṭukaḷ irukkak kāṇkiṟōm. ‘ūṉiṉic curukkiṉ uḷḷōḷi perukum’ eṉpār āṉṟōr. āṉāl iṅkē ūṉ curuṅka, uḷḷamumaṉṟō curuṅkiviṭṭatu. nāṉ cumār 50 āṇṭukaḷākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattōṭu toṭarpu uṭiyavaṉ. āṭcikkuḻu oṉṟu tavirttu piṟa aṉittilum paṅkukoṇṭavaṉ. cilavaṟṟil uṟuppiṉaṉākavum cilavaṟṟil talivaṉākavum iruntu paṇiyāṟṟi vantuḷḷēṉ. nāṉ ōyvu peṟṟa piṟakum cila kuḻukkaḷil iṭam peṟṟavaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭup piṟa palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷilum vaṭanāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum pala kuḻukkaḷil iṭam peṟṟavaṉ: akkālattil aṉittup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum āṟṟiya ceyalmuṟikaḷukkum iṉṟiya ceyalmuṟikaḷukkum ettaṉiyō vēṟupāṭukaḷiavi tāḻvuṟum vakiyil-irukkak kāṇkiṟēṉ. eṭuttukkāṭṭukku iraṇṭoṉṟu kāṭṭa niṉikkiṟēṉ. tavaṟāyiṉ poṟuttiṭa vēṇṭum!

kallūrikaḷukku aṭutta āṇṭiṟkup putiya pāṭaṅkaḷ vēṇṭiṉ ataṟku muntiya āṇṭu akṭōpar 31kkuḷ viṇṇappikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu viti. muṉpellām avvāṟu vanta viṇṇappaṅkaḷi āyntu, takuti aṟintu, avaṟṟi āyvataṟkeṉa āyvukkuḻukkaḷi (Inspection. Commission) caṉavari 31kkuḷ aṉuppap palkalik kaḻakam ēṟpāṭu ceyyum. avaṟṟiṉ parinturikaḷi āyntu, pipravari allatu mārccil naṭipeṟum āṭcik kuḻuviṉ kūṭṭam muṭiveṭuttu, mārccu muṭivukkuḷ avvak kallūrikaḷukku muṭiviṉit terivikkum. kallūri naṭattupavarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ēppiral, allatu mē tiṅkaḷil viḷamparaṅkaḷ ceytu kallūri tiṟakkumpōtu, cūṉ mātattil māṇavarkaḷic cērppar. inta muṟi māṇavarkaḷ cēravum āciriyarkaḷi niyamikkavum piṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷic ceyyavum ciṟanta muṟiyāka amintatu. āṉāl taṟpōtō, oṭṭu mottamāka ellākkallūrikaḷiyum iṇittu ētāvatu oru kāraṇamkāṭṭi, oru pāṭamum taramuṭiyātu eṉṟu aintu allatu āṟu mātaṅkaḷ, cila vēḷi kallūri tiṟanta piṟakum terivikkiṉṟaṉar. inta āṇṭu attakiya potuaṟikki oṉṟu 13-8-91il-ākasṭilkallūrikaḷ tiṟanta iraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷukkup piṟaku vantatu. inta vitikku vilakkaḷittum cila kallūrikaḷukkuc cila pāṭaṅkaḷ tarap peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpar. inta āṇṭu aṟiñar kuḻu aṟikki vantapiṟakum cepṭampar iṭiyil oru kallūrikkup putiya pāṭam-atuvum navampar ṭicamparil tērvu eḻuta vēṇṭiya paruvamuṟip pāṭam tarappeṟṟatām eṉṉē koṭumi. evvāṟu māṇavar teḷivu peṟuvar? ceṉṟa āṇṭum oru kallūrikku ākasṭu kaṭiciyil pāṭaṅkaḷ aḷittu, cepṭampar 15 variyil cērkkavum icivum tantaṉar. atuvum paruvattērvāyiṉ evvaḷavu tolliyuṟa vēṇṭiyirukkum eṉa eṇṇip pārppatilli. eppaṭiyum intak kūṭṭu muṟiyiṉṟi, taṉit taṉiyē ovvoru kallūrikkum (atuvē uriya celavut tokiyiṉiyum uṟuppiṉarkaḷukku uriya tokiyiṉiyum tantu viṭukiṉṟatu) taṉittaṉik kuḻu aṉuppi nēril ovvoṉṟiyum ārāyntu muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭum. inta āṇṭu oru kallūri kaṇippoṟi uyar vakuppu varum eṉṟa niliyil cumār mūṉṟu laṭcam rūpāykku kaṇippoṟi, kuḷir cātaṉap peṭṭi mēci nāṟkāli pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkuc celavu ceytatu. āyiṉum cepṭampar muṭiyum variyil ataṟkeṉa oru patilum palkalik kaḻakam aṉuppavilli. inta nili nīṭittāl kallūrikaḷ nili eṉṉavākum?

peyar māṟṟattukkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum kallūri toṭaṅkuvataṟkum aracu muṉ āṇi tantāltāṉ palkalik kaḻakam ceyalāṟṟamuṭiyum. āṉāl tamiḻaka aracu pāṭaṅkaḷittara orumuṟikku irumuṟi kaṭitam eḻutiyum tara maṟuttup palkalik kaḻakam taṉṉicciyāka iyaṅkukiṟatu. aracāṅkac cārpāka ataṉ kalvic ceyalarum kallūrik kalvi iyakkunarum niyamaṉa uṟuppiṉarum iruntum ippaṭip palkalik kaḻakam taṉṉicciyāka iyaṅkuvatu varuntutaṟkuriyatākum. varuntattakka maṟṟoṉṟiyum kūṟavēṇṭum. ceṉṟa āṇṭu 1990 mārccu ēppiralil tērvu eḻuti veṟṟi peṟṟa māṇavarukku inta āṇṭu mē variyil (1991-mē) uriya paṭṭac cāṉṟitaḻ vaḻaṅkavilli eṉiṉ appiḷḷikaḷiṉ nili eṉṉāvatu ‘Indian Express’ eṉṟa nāḷitaḻil (11-9-91) K.K. raṅkaṉ eṉṟa māṇavar (Varsity Lapses) attakiya avala niliyil tāṉ maṭṭumaṉṟi iṉṉum palar niliyiṉiyum cuṭṭi eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭitam palkalikkaḻaka avala niliyiṉi viḷakkukiṉṟatu.

palkalikkaḻaka aṟiñar kūṭṭamum pēraviyum piṟavum naṭipeṟumpōtu oruvar kāṇa nēriṭṭāl atu oru pōrkkaḷamākak kāṭci aḷippatik kāṇa muṭiyum eṉpar. āciriyar kuḻuviṉar eḻuppum viṉākkaḷukkuppatil colla muṭiyāmal atikārattil uḷḷavarkaḷ allal uṟuvatu paritāpamāka irukkum. pala kallūrikaḷ tavaṟu iḻikkiṉṟaṉa. atit taṭṭik kēṭka vēṇṭiya palkalik kaḻakamum kallūri iyakkakamum vāḷā iruppatōṭu, avaṟṟi ātarikkavum ceykiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ talivarkaḷē āṭcik kuḻuvil iruppatālō, aṉṟi vēṟu uyarmaṭṭa niliyil āṇi celuttuvatālō aṉṟi laṭcakkaṇakkil vāṅkum paṇatti vāri iṟippatālō vicārittut taṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ vāymūṭi meḷaṉiyākik kiṭakkiṟārkaḷ. nāṭṭuk kalvi nalamuṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam avarkaḷukku irukkavēṇṭum. āṉāl avarkaḷ tattam pataviyāl tam vaḷaṉip perukkik koḷḷa virumpukiṟārkaḷ. avaravarkaḷ taṟpōtuḷḷa patavikku varumuṉ ententa vaḻiyāl- yār yāri mitittum tuvittum vantōm eṉpatit tirumpip pārkkavēṇṭum. appōtu pala uṇmikaḷ avarkaḷukkē viḷaṅkum. aracāṅkamum ittakiya kaḷikaḷi uṭaṉ kaḷiya ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum. illiyāṉāl puriyōṭi, kalvi eṉum kārikikkē iṟuti yāttirikku ēṟpāṭu ceytuviṭuvārkaḷ. uḷpukuntu ārāyiṉ kaḷiya vēṇṭiyavi pala eṉa aṟiyamuṭiyum. pukuvār pukavilli, eṉavē vallāṉ vakuttatē vāykkāl eṉṟa vakiyil palkalikkaḻakam iyaṅkukiṟatu.

iṅkē iraṇṭoṉṟiyē cuṭṭiṉēṉ. iṉṉum pala uḷa. inta aḷavē amiyum eṉṟu karuti mēlē celkiṟēṉ. tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṟṟiya palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷō intiya nāṭṭup piṟa palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷō eppaṭi, iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati nāṉaṟiyēṉ. eṉiṉum intiya nāṭṭut toṉmiyāṉa oru palkalik kaḻakam ittakiya avala nilikkut taḷḷappaṭṭami aṟintu aṉivarum kavalippaṭuvatōṭu, aracāṅkattōṭu iṇittu itaṉi muṉṉip perumi peṟak kūṭiya neṟiyil āṟṟuppaṭutti āvaṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. ām! ataṉ makaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil allalpaṭṭu āṟṟātu vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.

taṉṉāṭcik kallūrikaḷ

palkalikkaḻaka elliyilēyē cila kallūrikaḷukkut taṉi urimi vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avi tamakkeṉavē taṉiyākap pāṭaṅkaḷi amittuk koṇṭu tērvu naṭattip paṭṭam peṟap palkalikkaḻakattukku aṉuppa vaḻi uṇṭu. inta niliyil taram uyarum eṉac cilarum taram tāḻum eṉac cilarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. paranta aḷavil tērvu nili iruntu muṉpiṉ aṟiyātār nēriya vakiyil viṭit tāḷkaḷit tiruttiṉāltāṉ māṇavar uṇmiyāṉa nili teriyum. aṟinta āciriyarō-aṉṟip payiṟṟiya āciriyarō viṉāttāḷ amittu viṭiyum tiruttum muṟi, nērmiyuṭaṉ amiyumāyiṉ cālac ciṟantatutāṉ. āṉāl anta nili palaviṭaṅkaḷil māṟupaṭukiṉṟatē! eṉavētāṉ palar attakiya kallūrikaḷi āyntu teḷivu kāṇa oru taṉikkuḻu amikka vēṇṭum eṉa vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar. appaṭiyē āṭcikkuḻuvil uḷḷavartam kallūrikaḷum carivara iyaṅkavilli eṉṟa kuṟipāṭṭu oliyum kātil viḻukiṟatu. ivaṟṟiyellām cariceytu oḻuṅkupaṭuttāviṭiṉ nāṭṭiṉ kalvi nalivuṟum. nāṭu nāṭāka irātu. nām maṉitarāka irōm! uṭaṉ āvaṉa kāṇa vēṇṭum.

taṉ nitik kallūrikaḷ

nāṭṭi ulakukku uṇrttum uyarkalvi nili naṉku pēṇap peṟal vēṇṭum. poṟiyiyal, maruttuvam, vivacāyam, kālnaṭi pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkeṉa aminta palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum ataṉic cārnta kallūrikaḷum kūṭat taram uyarttap peṟavēṇṭiya niliyil uḷḷaṉa. taṉiyār kallūrikaḷ-cuyanitik kallūrikaḷāka muḷittu ilaṭcakkaṇakkil poruḷ peṟṟum takka vacatikaḷ iṉṟic ceyalpaṭuvatākac cila ceytikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭil attakiya kallūrikaḷ nāḷtoṟum perukikkoṇṭē varukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē avaṟṟiṉ taram-nili kākkap peṟal vēṇṭum.

aṉittu intiyāvukkum orē vakiyāṉa kalvi muṟi amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṉivarum kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. paḷḷikaḷilē vēṇṭumāṉāl mānilaṅkaḷ toṟum moḻi, vāḻvu neṟi mutaliyaṉa māṟupaṭutal pōṉṟu kalvimuṟiyum māṟupaṭalām. āṉāl kallūrikaḷil-palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷil-orumi neṟi iyaṅka vēṇṭāmā? tamiḻakam tavira, piṟa mānilaṅkaḷil paṭṭa vakuppil mūṉṟu pāṭaṅkaḷ mukkiya pāṭaṅkaḷāka (Main) uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakattil oru pāṭam mukkiyamāṉatākavum ataṉ toṭarpāṉa maṟṟoṉṟu cārpunili peṟṟatākavum uḷḷaṉa. āḻntu cintippiṉ tamiḻakak kallūrip pāṭa muṟiyē ciṟantatu eṉat

tōṉṟum-uṇmiyum atuvē. ētēṉum oṉṟil tiṟam peṟiṉ, avaṉ piṉ, āciriyaṉākavō vēṟu vakiyilō vāḻkkiyil pukumpōtu atil teḷinta aṟivuṭiyavaṉāyp piṟari āṟṟuppaṭuttit tāṉum ceyalpaṭa muṭiyum. mūṉṟu eṉṟāl mūṉṟil oṉṟilum muḻumi peṟā niliyil oruvaṉ vāḻvu eppaṭic ciṟakkum? ataṉālēyē piṟa mānilaṅkaḷil payiṉṟu paṭṭm peṟṟavar āciriyar payiṟci peṟṟa pōtilum avarkaḷit tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarāka niyamikka añca vēṇṭi uḷḷatu. pala paḷḷikaḷ niyamippatum illi. inta niliyil intiya aracāṅkam-kalviyit taṉṉoṭu kaṭṭippiṭittuḷḷa aracāṅkam. oru potu vitiyi amikka vēṇṭum. appaṭiyē enta muṟik kalvi, iruppiṉum paḷḷikaḷil avi iyaṅkum mānilattiṉ moḻi mūṉṟil oṉṟō-iraṇṭil oṉṟō kaṭṭāyamāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉavum viti ceyya vēṇṭum. ittakiya potu vitikaḷ nāṭṭiṉ orumippāṭṭiṉi vaḷarppatōṭu moḻi aṟiviṉiyum kalvit teḷiviṉiyum tarum eṉpatu uṟuti.

potuvāka aracāṅkak kalvi niliyaṅkaḷik kāṭṭilum taṉiyār niliyaṅkaḷ-ārampap paḷḷi mutal kallūri variyil-cemmiyākac ceyalpaṭuvatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. aracāṅkak kallūrikaḷil pōtiya āciriyarkaḷi uriya kālattil niyamikkā nili uḷḷatu. aṭikkaṭi āciriyarkaḷi māṟṟum niliyum uḷḷatu. ivi perumpālum māṇavar nalaṉip pātikkātu irukkumō!

māṇavarkaḷiṭam peruntoki-entap peyarilē vāṅkiṉālum ataṟkēṟṟa vakiyil māṇavar tēvikaḷi niṟivuceyyiṉ-takka āciriyarkaḷi niyamittu-iṭiyaṟā vakiyil kalvi aḷittu, ceyalmuṟikaḷic cemmip paṭutti, veṟum ēṭṭuk kalviyoṭu nāṭṭup paṟṟum

nalloḻukkamum māṇavar uḷḷattil patiyumpaṭic ceyyiṉ naṉku payaṉ viḷiyum. orucilavāyiṉum ivvāṟu ceyalpaṭuvatāltāṉ, attakiya kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḷarcciyuṟukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷum paṇatti vāri iṟikkiṉṟaṉar. cuṭarviḷakkāyiṉum naṉṟāy viḷakkiṭat tūṇṭukōl oṉṟu vēṇṭum' eṉpatāl, iviyelām cemmiyāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉavā eṉak kaṇṭu, tavaṟiṉ nallāṟṟiṉ vaḻi tiruppi naṭattum vakiyil uyarnilikkuḻu-nalla paṇpāḷar aṭaṅkiya kuḻu oṉṟō palavō niyamikkap peṟal vēṇṭum. avi vaḻikāṭṭa nāṭṭiṉ kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ nēriya pātiyil aṭi eṭuttu vikkumāṉāl tamiḻakam pārata nāṭṭil maṭṭumiṉṟi ulakilēyē uyarntu viḷaṅkum eṉpatu uṟuti. virintu ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭum.

kāl nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam maṭṭumē tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntatu. appōtirunta cila kuṟipāṭukaḷip paṟṟip ‘palkalik kaḻakap pārvikku' eṉṟu talippiṭṭu oru kaṭṭuri eḻutiṉēṉ. appōtu eṅkaḷ vīṭṭukku varum aṉpar tiru kaviñar kaṇṇatācaṉ avarkaḷ avar tam teṉṟal itaḻukku eṉṉik kaṭṭuri eḻutac colluvār oru cila veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār eṉa niṉikkiṟēṉ. ik kaṭṭuriyum avar kaṇṇil paṭṭu, avar itaḻilō vēṟu itaḻilō veḷivantatu, ati appaṭiyē palkalik kaḻakattic cērnta aṉpar aṉṟiya tuṇivēntar tiru. ilaṭcumaṇacāmi mutaliyār avarkaḷiṭam kāṭṭi viḷakkiṉārām.

uṭaṉē tiru. mutaliyār avarkaḷ eṉṉi varac coṉṉār. kāliyil avar vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟēṉ. eṉṉi uṭkāra vittu nāṉ eḻutiyatip paṟṟik kūṟi, palkalikkaḻakam pōṉṟa periya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil,ittakiya tavaṟukaḷ varuvatu iyaṟki. avaṟṟic cuṭṭik kāṭṭiya uṅkaḷip pārāṭṭukiṟēṉ. ittakiya cuṭṭik kāṭṭalkaḷ

tāṉ naṭpukku aṭiyāḷam. uṅkaḷukku naṉṟi eṉṟu kūṟi vāḻtti aṉuppi vittār. eṉakku ‘nakutaṟ poruṭ ṭaṉṟu naṭṭal' eṉṟa kuṟaḷ niṉivukku vantatu. avari vāḻttiṉēṉ.

āṉāl iṉṟu nāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷati palkalik kaḻakattil uḷḷavarkaḷ eppaṭik koḷvārkaḷō! nalla uḷḷaṅkaḷ nakutaṟporuṭṭaṉṟu naṭṭal, mikutikkaṇ mēṟceṉṟu iṭittal poruṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟaḷ vaḻi ivaṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷvar eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. eppaṭiyāvatu eṉṉi āḷākkiya palkalikkaḻakam paḻiya niliyi aṭiya vēṇṭum eṉpatē eṉ āci. ati iṉṟuḷḷavarkaḷ niṟivēṟṟuvārkaḷ eṉavum nampukiṟēṉ.

palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷilō kallūrikaḷilō māṇavarkaḷic cērkkumpōtu, taram eṇṇap peṟa vēṇṭum. poṟiyiyal kallūri, maruttuvak kallūri mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku 90, 95 eṉak kāṇum niliyil piṟa kallūrikaḷukkuk kuṟintatu aṟupatāvatu 60 irukka vēṇṭāmā? ‘pi. pi. ē.’ vakuppil cērvataṟku maṭṭum ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakam attakiya vitiyiṉi amittuḷḷatu. atēpōṉṟu piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷukkum amittāl evvaḷavu ēṟṟam peṟum.‘ Eligible for College Course’ eṉṟu muṟkālattiya paḷḷi iṟuti vakuppiṉ cāṉṟitaḻkaḷil acciṭap peṟṟirukkum nallavēḷi taṟpōtiya cāṉṟitaḻkaḷil attakiya toṭar illātatu makiḻccikkuriyatē. eṉiṉum muppattintu vāṅkit tēṟiṉāṉ eṉṟāl ammāṇavaṉ kallūriyil cērat takuti peṟṟavaṉākiṉṟāṉ. periya iṭattu cipāricu iruntālō allatu perum paṇam koṭuttālō avaṉukkum kallūriyil iṭam kiṭittu viṭukiṟatu. tāḻttappaṭṭōrukkum otukkappeṟṟōrukkum piṟa toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷukku iruppatu pōṉṟu ōraḷavu caluki taruvatil tavaṟilli. mēlum ettaṉi muṟi paṭiyeṭuttu, ovvoru pāṭamākat tēṟiṉālum

avaṉukkum iṭam tara vēṇṭiya nili kallūrikku uṇṭākiṉṟatu. inta niliyil kallūriyiṉ taram eṅkē nāṭṭap peṟum? mēlum atuvariyil tamiḻil ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum payiṉṟa māṇavar palar, āṅkilam payiṟci moḻiyāka uḷḷa kallūrikaḷil iṭam vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl aracāṅkamē naṭattum orucila kallūrikaḷ tavirttu, perumpālāṉa kallūrikaḷ āṅkilattiyē payiṟci moḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ paṇipuriyum āciriyarkaḷuḷ cilar tamiḻ vāṭiyē aṟiyātavarkaḷ; cilar tamiḻē puriyātavarkaḷ. cilar tamiḻil collittaruvatit tāḻvāka eṇṇupavarkaḷ. inta niliyiṉāl āṅkilamē payiṟṟu moḻiyākiṟatu. aracāṅkam inta vakiyil karuttirutti, tam kallūrikaḷil tamiḻp payiṟṟumoḻi vakuppukaḷi ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭum. tamiḻil paḷḷiyil payiṉṟōm atē marapiṉittāṉ kallūriyil piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉa marapu ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl pala māṇavar moḻiyaṟiyātu taṭumāṟit tērvukaḷil poruḷ viḷaṅka māṭṭātu iṭarppaṭṭu tōlviyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. inta avala nilikku muṟṟup puḷḷi vikka aracāṅkam uṭaṉ takka ākka naṭavaṭikki eṭukkavēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ.

maṟṟoṉṟum kāṇal vēṇṭum. pāṭattil ‘10+2+3' eṉa māṟṟiya pōtu aracāṅkap paṇi puripavarkkeṉṟē (Vocational Course) āciriyap payiṟci mutaliyaṉa pukuttap peṟṟaṉa. āciriyap payiṟci taṟpōtu kiviṭap peṟṟatu. toḻil kalanta kalvi payiṉṟavar aracāṅka eḻuttar, taṭṭaccāḷar pōṉṟa patavikaḷukkuc cellaveṉṟē atu amikkappeṟṟatu. āṉāl taṟpōtu, atil payiṉṟavarukkum pattil oru pakuti iṭam tarum nili uṇṭākiyuḷḷatu. iti uṇṭākkiyavar yār eṉṟu teriyavilli. eṉiṉum inta muṟi nīkkap peṟal vēṇṭum. aracup paṇiyāḷar tērvāṇiyam ittakiya māṇavarkaḷukku

mutaliṭam aḷittu, aracāṅka uttiyōkaṅkaḷ taravaḻi kāṇavēṇṭum. ataṟkum cila tiruttaṅkaḷ tēvi. anta iruvakuppukaḷip paṟṟi eḻutum pōtu atupaṟṟi virivākak kāṇalām.

kalviyiṉi mānila, mattiya aracukaḷōṭu oṉṟi iṇittamiyiṉ pala mattiya paḷḷikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḷarntaṉa. appaṭiyē cila palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷum mattiya araciṉ āṇi vaḻiyē iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. putuvip palkalikkaḻakam attakiya palkalik kaḻakamē. inta nili vēṇṭattakāta oṉṟu. aṇmiyil acām mānilattil iru putiya mattiya palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ toṭaṅkat tilli aracu muyaṉṟum etirppiṉ kāraṇattāl kiviṭappaṭṭatu eṉpatu pattirikic ceytikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvatoṉṟu. atu maṭṭumiṉṟi attakiya palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷukkut taṉic calukiyum taṉitta uyarniliyum taruvatu vēṇṭap peṟuvataṉṟu. tēviyaṟṟa oṉṟit tōṟṟuvittu, ataṟkeṉat taṉic caluki tantu uyarttuvatu mānila makkaḷik kuṟittu matippiṭuvatākum. vēṇṭumāyiṉ mānilaṅkaḷ amitta palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷukku atika māṉiyam koṭuttu, aṅkaṅkē uḷḷa kaḷikaḷik kaḷintu, kāval tuṟiyil tēviyāṉapōtu mattiya kāval paṭiyiṉi aṉuppuvatu pōṉṟu, mattiya uyar maṭṭakkuḻu oṉṟi amittu, tavaṟukaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, tiruttam ceyyalām. appōtu palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, mānila aracukaḷumkūṭa añcic ceyalpaṭum. ataṉāl nāṭṭil kalvi naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟum.

mēli nāṭukaḷil orē palkalikkaḻakattē palavakip payiṟci vakuppukaḷ- paṭṭa vakuppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vēṟu pala kallūrikaḷ iṇikkappeṟavilli. anta niliyil nam nāṭṭilum cila palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum avaṟṟiṉ taram, mēli nāṭukaḷil kāṇpatu pōṉṟu attuṇi vakiyil ciṟantatāka illi. tamiḻ nāṭṭil attakiya palkalik kaḻakattil payiṉṟu uyarnta niliyil ciṟappunili-mutal niliyil paṭṭam peṟṟavarāyiṉum avarkaḷik kallūri āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikkak kūṭātu eṉṟu, tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē maṟṟoru palkalik kaḻakam āṇiyiṭukiṟatu. itu evvaḷavu kēvalam eṉa eṇṇip pārppatilli: orē māṉiyak kuḻuviṉ kīḻ, orē aracariṉ .(Chancellor – Governor) kīḻ iyaṅkum oru palkalik kaḻakatti maṟṟoru kaḻakam matippatilli eṉpatu veṭkak kēṭu allavā! atē vēḷiyil avvāṟu matikkāta palkalik kaḻakattē kaṭalaṉiya kuṟikaḷ malintuḷḷati avarkaḷ uṇarvatilli. iyēcunātar kūṟiya ‘uṉ kaṇṇi lirukkiṟa uttaratti eṭuttup pōṭṭu maṟṟavaṉ kaṇṇilirukkum turumpip paṟṟik karutu’ eṉṟu kūṟiya nīti iṅku eṉ niṉivukku varukiṟatu.

atē vēḷiyil taṉ kīḻ uḷḷa taṉakku vēṇṭiya cila kallūrikaḷukkut taṉṉāṭci urimi vaḻaṅki, avaṟṟuḷ pala maṉam pōṉapōkkil ceyal iyaṟṟuvatiyum tērvu naṭattuvatiyum kaṇṭu koḷḷāmal paṭṭam vaḻaṅkum oru palkalik kaḻakattip paṟṟi aḻuvatā cirippatā eṉpatu teriyavilli. aṇmiyil attakiya taṉṉāṭcik kallūrikaḷip paṟṟi ārāyavum, tēviyāyiṉ avaṟṟip paḻiya muṟiyil palkalik kaḻakattōṭu iṇikkavum muyaṟcikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap pōvtāka aṟikiṟēṉ. iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil attakiya kallūri oṉṟiṉ ceyalpāṭṭiṉāl pala iṉṉalkaḷ uṇṭāṉati nāḷēṭukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracāṅkam virintu ittakiya ceyalkaḷittirutta nalaṉ kāṇac ceyalpaṭṭāl nāṭṭukkum nallatu; ataṟkum nallatu:

tērvu muṟi paṟṟiyum muṉpē cila kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. iṅkē oṉṟi eṭuttuk kāṭṭa virumpukiṟēṉ. tērvukaḷi naṭattum poṟuppiṉip palkalik kaḻakamē koṇṭiruntālum, ati naṭattat taṉi niṟuvaṉam oṉṟi:amittu ataṉiṭam oppaṭikka vēṇṭum. atu. pativāḷar aṭiyiṉ kīḻō vēṟu vakiyilō aṭakkamākātu taṉi niliyil iyaṅkavēṇṭum. tērvukkuriyōri- viṉāttāḷ tayārippōr- tiruttuvōr paṭṭiyalip palkalik kaḻakam taralām. tērvukaḷum nāṉ muṉṉamē kuṟittapaṭi māṇavar paṭitta kallūriyilēyē naṭipeṟuvatu tavaṟu.• uṇmiyāṉa aṟivuṭiyārik kaṇṭaṟiya atu utavātu. itupaṟṟi muṉṉarē viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. ivvāṟu tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa tērvuk kuḻuvē taṉi ātikkam peṟṟa kallūrikaḷ uṭpaṭa aṉittuk kallūrikaḷukkum uriya tērvi naṭattavēṇṭum.

nāḷitaḻ vaḻiyē (8.11.91-intiyaṉ ekspiras) oru māṇavar muṟiyākap paṇam kaṭṭit tērvu eḻutiyum patiṉāṉku mātaṅkaḷukku mēlāka avar tērvu muṭiviṉit tarāta kāraṇattāl, avar vaḻakkiṭa, uṇmi kaṇṭa nītimaṉṟam avarukkup palkalikkaḻakam irupatteṭṭāyiram (28,000) naṭṭīṭākat taravēṇṭum eṉa āṇiyiṭṭateṉpati aṟiya varunta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatallavā! ittakiya kuṟikaḷ tavirkkap peṟavēṇṭum. muṟiyāṉa kavaṉippum mēṟpārviyum iruppiṉ ivi nikaḻa vaḻi illiyē!.

nāṭṭuppuṟa allatu kirāmappuṟap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum nāṭṭil cila uḷḷaṉa. aviyum mānila araciṉ ellikkuḷḷākavē varavēṇṭum. kirāmaṅkaḷip paṟṟi aṟiyum vāyppu mānila aracukkē uṇṭu. aṅku vāḻum makkaḷukku uriya tēviyāṉa muṟiyil kalvi aḷikkum neṟi aṟintu āṟṟuppaṭutti ippalkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭum muṟi mānila aracukkuttāṉ puriyum. kūṭumāyiṉ attakiya palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ iyaṅkum māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷarit talivarākakkoṇṭu kaṟṟaṟinta nallavarkaḷ aminta oru kuḻuviṉiyum ataṉ mēṟpārvikku eṉa amikkalām. veṟum pāṭaṅkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi. am makkaḷ vāḻkkikkut tēviyāṉa aṉittum aṅkē niṟivu ceyyap peṟal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl veṟum peyaraḷavil tāṉ avi iyaṅkum. inta nili mukkiyamākak kaṇṭu pōṟṟappeṟal vēṇṭum.

palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ tām iyaṅkum antantac camutāya amippiṟku ēṟpa iyaṅkavēṇṭum. eṅkō yārō vakutta pāṭaṅkaḷ-tēviyaṟṟa teḷivaṟṟa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ-eṉṟō evarō vakutta pāṭamuṟikaḷ eṉṟa vakiyil iyaṅkātu avvappōtu atic cārnta camutāyattukku ēṟṟa pāṭaṅkaḷit tiṟaṉṟintu-muṟi aṟintu-cilavaṟṟit teḷivikkum vakiyaṟintu pōṟṟi amikkavēṇṭum.

eṉavē palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ aviavi vāḻum cūḻalukkum camutāyattukkum ēṟṟavakiyil payaṉpaṭal nalam payappatākum. atuvē nāṭu vaḷara vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum amiyavēṇṭum.

ārampak kalvi mutal uyariya 'ṭākṭar' paṭṭampeṟum kalvivari oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu toṭarpu koṇṭatākavē amiyavēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil etiyō paṭittu, mēniliyil tēviyaṟṟatit tērnteṭuttu veṟṟi kaṇṭu, vēṟu etuvum kiṭikkātatāl kiṭitta etilō cērntu eppaṭiyō paṭṭampeṟumnili paṭippavarukkum nallataṉṟu; nāṭṭukkum nallataṉṟu. kōṭi kōṭiyākap paṇamtāṉ vīṇākum. ivvāṟu payiṉṟu, paṭṭam peṟutal uttiyōkam peṟuvataṟkākavē eṉṟa eṇṇamum makkaḷ maṉattil uṇṭākakkūṭātu. uttiyōkattip paṭṭappaṭippuṭaṉ iṇittup pārkkalākātu eṉa muṉṉarē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. nāṉ mēnilip paḷḷiyil cuṭṭikkāṭṭa iruppatupōṉṟu aṅkē taṉiyē amintuḷḷa kalvi muṟiyil payiṉṟōrē aracāṅka, piṟa uttiyōkap paṭippiṉik kaṟṟavarē aracāṅkattum piṟaviṭattum uttiyōkam peṟattakkavar. piṉ avaravar paṇiyāṟṟum nilikkēṟpa vēṟu tērvukaḷil (Departmental test) veṟṟipeṟṟumēlē uyaralām. inta niliyil kallūriyiṉ kaṉam kuṟiyum. araciṉ paṇamum miccamākum. camutāyamum vīṇpoḻutil kālam kaḻittōm eṉak kavalippaṭavēṇṭi irukkātu. inta vakiyil palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷi uṭaṉē tirutti amittal avaṟṟi uruvākkum mānila aracukaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākum eṉa uṇarntu avi ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ.





* * *



yātāṉum nāṭāmāl ūrāmāl eṉoruvaṉ



cāntuṇiyum kallāta vāṟu (kuṟaḷ)

immi payakkumāl īyak kuṟiviṉṟāl

tammi viḷakkumāl tām uḷarāk kēṭiṉṟāl

emmi ulakattum yāmkāṇēm kalvipōl



mammar aṟukkum maruntu (nālaṭiyār)



* * *





⁠﻿3. mēnili vakuppu (+2)

* * *





tamiḻakak kalvittuṟiyilē, patiṉoṉṟu, paṉṉiraṇṭu ākiya iruvakuppukaḷaṭaṅkiya (+2) mēnilik kalviyē ciṟappāka amiyavēṇṭiyatākum. pattām vakuppu vari ellārum perumpālum antanta ūriliruntē paṭikka vacatikaḷ perukivarukiṉṟaṉa. anta vakuppukaḷil perumpālum tāymoḻi mūlamē kalvi kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟatu. perunakaraṅkaḷil pala paḷḷikaḷil āṅkilam payiṟṟumoḻiyāka iruppiṉum pala paḷḷikaḷil tamiḻē payiṟṟumoḻiyāka uḷḷatu. piḷḷikaḷ tāymoḻi vaḻiyē eḷitil kaṟṟu muṉṉēṟa vaḻi ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum atuvari payiṟṟu moḻi etuvāyiṉum-ilavacak kalviyākavē uḷḷatu. āyiṉum antap pattām vakuppilēyē nūṟṟukku 60 allatu 70kkumēl tērcci peṟuvatu aritāka uḷḷatu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam āciriyarkaḷ cilar tiṟampṭ iyaṅkāmiyum māṇavarkaḷukkut tēviyāṉa upakaraṇaṅkaḷōṭu karumpalaki uṭpaṭa nalla kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷō cūḻniliyō illāmiyumākum. eppaṭiyō antap pattām vakuppiṉit tāṇṭiyapiṉ, orucilar tavirttup perumpālōr-pātikkumēl patiṉōrām vakuppil cērkiṉṟaṉar. appaṭic cērumpōtu perumpālum āṅkilam payiṟṟumoḻiyāka amiyum paḷḷikaḷē atikam uḷḷaṉa. cila vakuppukaḷil tamiḻ payiṟṟumoḻiyāka iruppiṉum ellāp paḷḷikaḷilum āṅkilam payiṟṟumoḻiyāka uḷḷatu. attakiya vakuppukaḷukkup paḷḷik kaṭṭaṇamum uṇṭu (ciṟutokiyē). aracāṅka muṟiyil māṉiyampeṟum paḷḷikaḷpōka, māṉiyampeṟāta meṭrikulēṣaṉ paḷḷi pōṉṟavi, mattiyapaḷḷi ākiyavaṟṟil payilum māṇavarkaḷ pattām
