intiyā urimi peṟṟu nāṟapattintu āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutē ellārukkum (3-15) vayatuc kalvi aṟivu taravēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyil nāṭu īṭupaṭṭu. iruntāl iṉṟu 50 vayatukku uṭpaṭṭavar aṉivarum kaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruppar. iṉṟu maruttuvap paṭṭam peṟupavar aracāṅkp paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉāl ōrāṇṭu kirāmaṅkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti koṇṭu varap pōvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cātāraṇap paṭṭam peṟa virumpuvarkaḷukkum paṭippu muṭintapiṉ ōrāṇṭu kirāmattil paṇipurintāltāṉ paṭṭam peṟamuṭiyum eṉṟu ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum eṉac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. intak karuttiṉittāṉ, nāṉ muṉṉarē kuṟittapaṭi, 1947il cutantiram peṟṟa uṭaṉē intiyā mutalcaṭṭamāka inta muṟikku ēṟpa, oru caṭṭam iyaṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ‘intiya mutaṟ caṭṭam’ eṉṟa nūlil eḻutiṉēṉ. atil paṭṭam peṟupavar ōr āṇṭil kirāmaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu, aṅkētaṅki, avarkaḷoṭu paḻaki, avartam ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ aṟintu, tuyartuṭittu, kalvi aḷittu, toḻilperuka vaḻikaṇṭu eṉṉeṉṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉa viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. nāṭakamaṇi T.K. caṇmukam atip pārāṭṭit tām ataṉi nāṭakamāka naṭatta virumpuvatākavum kūṟiṉār. iraṇṭoru paḷḷikaḷ naṭittaṉa. annūlukku aṉṟiya kalvi amiccarāka irunta māṇpumiku tiru. paktavatcalam avarkaḷum pārāṭṭi muṉṉuri tantuḷḷārkaḷ. eṉiṉum inta muṟi ittaṉi āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟap peṟavilli. immuṟi aṉṟē vaḻakkattil, vantirukkumāyiṉ kirāmaṅkaḷil kalvi kaṟṟōr eṇṇikki mika atikamāka vaḷarntirukkum. kallātār illi eṉṟa niliyē uruvāki irukkum. āṉāl iṉṟu anta nili illi. iṉṟiya niliyil eḻuttaṟiyār nili paṟṟi aracāṅka veḷiyīṭṭiṉiyē iṅkē tara niṉikkiṉṟēṉ. nāṭṭiṉ avalaniliyiṉiyum, mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷiyum. ceyya vēṇṭiyatāya kālap paṇiyiṉiyum atu viḷakkuvati ōraḷavu kāṇa iyalum. ulakil uḷḷa kallātār eṇṇikkiyil pāti aḷavu nam nāṭṭiltāṉ irukkum niliyi eṇṇi ovvoru intiyaṉum varunti veṭkappaṭa vēṇṭāmā? eṅkō orucārār muṉṉēṟi viṭṭatākavum perumpālōr paṭittatākavum kūṟik koṇṭu, ēḻikaḷ-kirāma makkaḷ-peṇkaḷ ivarkaḷuḷ perumpālōr paṭikkātirukkum niliyiṉi eṇṇip, pārppatilli. atilum iṉṟu nakaraṅkaḷil vaḷarum āṅkila mōkattiṉ āḻattil putintu kiṭakkum mākkaḷ, tammip pōl intiya makaṉāka-makaḷāka vāḻum eṇṇaṟṟa makkaḷi eṇṇip pārppatilli; māṟāka ēḷaṉam ceyvar. aracāṅka muyaṟcikaḷum avvaḷavākap payaṉ taruvatilli. anta aracāṅka aṟikkiyiṉi iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ.

COMMITTEES PERSPECTIVE

7-2-1. The Resolution setting up the committee to review NPE, inter alia, states:

“Despite efforts at social and economic development since attinment of Independence a majority of our people continue to remin deprived or education, which is one of the basic needs for human development. It is also a matter of grave concern that our people comprise 50 per cent of the world's illiterate, and large sections of children have to go without acceptable level of primary education. Government accords the highest priority, to education-both as a human right and as the means for bringing about a transformation towards a more humane and enlightened society. There is need to make education an effective instrument for securing a status of equality for women, and persons belonging to the backward classes and minorities. Moreover, it is essential to give a work and employment orientation to education

POST POLICY IMPLEMENTATION

7-3-0 In pursuances of the Policy, a National Literacy Mission was established in 1988. In quantitative terms, the Mission seeks to impart functional literacy to 80 million illiterate persons in 15-35 age group: 80 million by 1990 and an additional 50 million by 1995. The emphasis of the Mission is not on mere numbers but on attinment of certin pre - determined norms and parameters of literacy, numeracy, functionality and awareness. Under this programme 2,84,000 centres are functioning in the country with an estimated involvement of 84 lakh adult learners (about 35 lakh men and 49 lakh women). Six lakh literacy kits have been delivered to students, volunteers. Over 30,000 Jan Shikshan Nilayams have been sanctioned to . provide post - Literacy programmes. Over 800 voluntary Agencies have also been involved in the process. A Mass Campign under NLM was lunched by the then Prime Minister in May, 1988. Similar campigns were lunched by 24 States and UTs on the same date and after. The Director General. NLM in his presentation before the . NPERC stated that the Programme has suffered due to shortage of funds. As aginst the barest minimum requirement of funds amounting to Rs. 189 crores during 1989-90, the actual amount provided was of the order of 76.17 crores. The position was somewhat improved in 1990-91 when a sum of Rs.96 crores was provided, considering the magnitude of the problem of illiteracy the large uncover area and the need for mobilisation (A total of Rs.227 crores was spent under NLM during the years 1987-88 to 1989-90). Much more funds are required even for implementing a purely volunteer-based, campign-oriented plan of action than what has been provided. (Towards an Enlightened and Humane Society-Pages 198 & 194)

nam nāṭṭilēyē maliyāḷa mānilattil nūṟṟukku nūṟu kaṟṟavarkaḷ irukkum pōtu ovvoru mānilamum attakiya muyaṟcikaḷi ēṉ mēṟkoḷḷalākātu. , oru cila mānilaṅkaḷ ōraḷavu muyalkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum pōtiya aḷavu illi eṉpatiyē nāṭṭunili kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉip poṟuttavariyil kōvi māvaṭṭattil ammāvaṭṭa āṭciyar talimiyil oru eḻuttaṟivippu iyakkam amintateṉa aṟikiṟōm. (tiru. irāmamūrttiyiṉ veḷiyīṭu 196) 5.25 ilaṭcam eḻuttaṟiyā makkaḷukku utava, ati 21 pakutikaḷākap pirittu cumār 50,000 taṉiyār utavi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ vaḻiyē ceyalāṟṟi 1990 ēppiral mutal 1992 mārccu muṭiyumuṉ, ammāvaṭṭattiyē muḻutum kaṟṟa māvaṭṭamākka muyalvatāka aracāṅkak kuṟippu kāṭṭukiṟatu. eṉiṉum aṅkēyum avarkaḷ niṉittapaṭi payaṉviḷiyavilli eṉṟē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ām! kaṭanta nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷil iḷam piḷḷikaḷ (5-14) kaṭṭāyamākap payila vēṇṭiya tiṭṭattiṉi eppāṭupaṭṭēṉum niṟivēṟṟi iruntāl iṉṟu inta avalanili irātu allavā! taṟpōtu tamiḻnāṭṭil puttuṇarcci arumpiyuḷḷatu. putukkōṭṭi māvaṭṭattiṉip pūraṇakkalvi aṟi māvaṭṭamākka muṉintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟa ceyti aṇmiyil veḷivantatu. kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭam ēṟakkuṟiya niṟivu eytum niliyil uḷḷatu eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭil pala niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ tāmē muṉvantu, inta eḻuttaṟivu iyakkattil paṅku koṇṭu pāṭupaṭa muṉikiṉṟatu eṉak kēṭṭaṟikiṟōm. paḷḷiyilum kallūriyilum payilum māṇavarkaḷ tāmākavē muṉvantu ūrtōṟum ceṉṟu inta ākkappaṇikku āvaṉa ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṟōm. iṉṟiya mānila aracum inta vakiyil mattiya araciṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉ pala kōṭi celaviṭṭu inteḻuttaṟivu iyakkattiṉic ceyalpaṭuttukiṉṟatu eṉpatik kēṭka makiḻcci piṟakkiṉṟatu. aracāṅkam pala āṇṭukaḷāka itaṟkeṉak kalvittuṟiyil oru taṉippirivē amittu, ataṟkeṉat taṉi iyakkunariyum irukka vittu, ūrtōṟum ceyalpaṭuvakiyil pala atikārikaḷi niyamittu āvaṉa ceytu varukiṉṟatu. ovvoru pañcāyattu elliyilum taṉi aluvalarkaḷ niyamikkap peṟṟu, avarkaḷ ūrtoṟum ceṉṟu mutiyōr kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ amittup pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mutiyavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya palaki, puttakam, karumpalaki, pōṉṟavaṟṟi iṉāmāka aḷittu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. mutalil oru cilar cērntālum mella mella. avaruḷ palar viṭṭu vilakukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa kuralē kēṭkiṟatu. ‘vantu paṭippataṟku eṉakkeṉṉa taruvāy’ eṉṟa kēḷviyum kēṭkap peṟukiṟatām! eṅkō oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil tāmākavē valiya vantu kaṟkum periyavarkaḷ-eḻuttaṟivaṟṟavar irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. avarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷum vakiyil paṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakka nūlkaḷ, iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum aracāṅkam tarukiṟatu. avarkaḷukku eppoḻutu ōyvu kiṭikkumō appōtu vakuppukaḷi naṭattavum tayārāka uḷḷatu. eṉiṉum makkḷ ārvam kāṭṭavilli eṉkiṉṟaṉar. iḷampiḷḷikaḷ paṭikkum paḷḷikaḷilēyē anta nili eṉa aṟikiṟōm pakal uṇavum pālum paḻamum piṟavum aḷikkiṉṟa kāraṇattāl tāṉ paḷḷiyil taṟpōtu atikamāṉa piḷḷikaḷ payilkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aracāṅkamē takaval tarukiṉṟatē. inta nili ellām pōkka vaḻi illiyā! maruntilliyā aracāṅka muṉippu pōtātā! paṇiyāḷar muyaṟci paṟṟākkuṟiyil amikiṉṟatā? teriyavilliyē.

1981 makkaḷ kaṇakkuppaṭi nam nāṭṭil payilāta āṇkaḷ 53% ākavum peṇkaḷ 75% iruntaṉar. ippōtō inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilō nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiyapaṭi, ulakak kallātār eṇṇikkiyil pāti intiyāvil irukkum eṉkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam makkaḷ itil pōtiya kavaṉam celuttāmiyētāṉ. nāṭṭil oṉṟum kallātavaṉ-kittoḻil viṉiñaṉ oru nāḷikku 50, 60 eṉṟum ataṟku mēlum campātikkiṟāṉ. peṇkaḷum appaṭiyē irupattintu, muppatu eṉa nāḷtōṟum campātikkiṉṟaṉar. iḷampiḷḷikaḷum kūṭa 15 vayatil-iṉṉum 10 vayatil kūṭa 10 allatu 15, 20 vari nāṭkūli peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟic cēmittu vāḻkkiyiṉic cemmippaṭuttukiṟārkaḷā eṉpatu vēṟu. āṉāl ivvāṟu peṟum varuvāyiṉi viṭuttu, paṭikka varuvatu kaṭiṉam. ‘paṭittāl eṉṉa itiviṭa atikamāka varap pōkiṟatā?’ eṉṟa kēḷvi avarkaḷ uḷḷattil eḻukiṉṟatu. mēlum palar māli vēḷikaḷil kuṭikkum pōtip poruḷukkum aṭimiyākiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭu vēlikku uṭpaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iravilō aṉṟi viṭiyaṟ kāliyilō camiyal ceytu, uṇṭu. vēlikkup pōkum pōtum matiya uṇavukkāka uṭaṉ eṭuttuc ceṉṟāltāṉē nāḷ muḻutum uḻikka muṭiyum. inta niliyil avarkaḷ paṭippateṅkē? piḷḷikaḷ varalām. avarkaḷi nēriya vaḻiyil āṟṟuppuṭutta vēṇṭāmā? aracāṅkam inta avalaniliyip pōkka ētētō vaḻivakikaḷik kaṇṭu koṇṭutāṉ varukiṟatu. āyiṉum avaṟṟic ceyalpaṭuttuvatil uḷḷa kuṟipāṭukaḷ kaḷiyappeṟal vēṇṭum.

periyōrkaḷukku eṉa ōrāṇṭō īrāṇṭō kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ payila varuvōr kuṟintatu pātināṭkaḷāvatu variṉ ōraḷavu eḻuttaṟivu peṟa muṭiyum. āṉāl ataṟkum pañcam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. ētō maṟṟavar vaṟpuṟuttalukkākac cērntatākavum vēli veṭṭi aṟṟavar ceyal itu eṉṟum niṉikkiṉṟavar iṉṟum palar uḷḷaṉar. inta niṉiviṉi māṟṟa vēṇṭum. ōraḷavāvatu paṭittālaṉṟic camūkattil uyara muṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇmi avarkaḷ uḷḷattil uṇara vaḻivaki kāṇa vēṇṭum. uṭaṉ ceyalāṟṟiṉ takka payaṉ viḷiyum.

oru kālattil varikaṭṭupavarukkuttāṉ caṭṭacapit tērtal vākkurimi eṉṟiruntatu. piṉ eḻutap paṭikkat, terintavarkaḷukku ellām vākkurimi uṇṭu eṉṟu viti vakukkappeṟṟatu. akkāla nikaḻccikaḷi nāṉ eṇṇip pārkkiṟēṉ. kirāmaṅkaḷilum nakaraṅkaḷilum eḻuttu vācaṉiyē aṟiyāta palar. eppaṭiyāvatu paṭikka vēṇṭum-ki eḻuttāvatu pōṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu muyaṉṟu, ataṉ vaḻiyē vākkāḷar ākum takuti peṟa muyaṉṟaṉar. antac cila āṇṭukaḷil kaṇakku eṭuttiruntāl eḻuttaṟivu iyakka vaḷarcci naṉku viḷakkum. ittaṉikkum antak kālattil aracāṅka utaviyō-aṭṭiṟ cālikaḷō ilavaca nūl utaviyō piṟavō illi. palar yāriyāvatu aṇṭik kaṟṟaṉar. anta nili māṟi urimi peṟṟapiṉ ellārum innāṭṭu maṉṉarāka uyarntu viṭṭamiyiṉ, paṭittavar paṭiyātavar -celvar vaṟiyar yāvarāyiṉum patiṉeṭṭu nirampap peṟṟāl vākkaḷarākavum, ataṉ vaḻic caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarākavum-ēṉ?-nāṭāḷum amiccarākavum kūṭa ākalām eṉṟa nili vantuviṭṭamiyiṉ paṭippil kavaṉam kuṟintatu. paṭikkāta mēti eṉṟa paṭṭamum vaḻaṅkap peṟum-eppaṭiyum campātikkalām eṉṟa niliyum kūṭavē cērntuviṭum piṉ mutiyōr kalvi eppaṭi vaḷarum? inta avalanili jaṉanāyaka nāṭṭiṉi eppaṭit talitūkka vikkum? itaṉālēyē nāṭṭil palvēṟu pirivukaḷum cāti camayac caṇṭikaḷum piṟakoṭumikaḷum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattil kallātavar kaṟṟavar coṟkēṭṭu aṭaṅki nallavaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭu vāḻntaṉar eṉak kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.

1977-78il ārampitta, nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiya aracāṅkac cārpuṭiya eḻuttaṟiviyakkam-mutiyōr kalvi muṟi pala vakiyil iṭarppaṭṭu cilaviṭaṅkaḷil toṭaṅkiya paḷḷikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭu payaṉaṟṟa niliyil, 1989il putiya muyaṟciyiṉi aracāṅkam mēṟkoṇṭatu. ipputiya muṟi kēraḷam, kōvā, putuvi, kujarāt ākiya mānilaṅkḷil naṉku naṭipeṟṟup payaṉum viḷintuḷḷatu eṉpar. (p. 198). itaṉ vaḻi, pala taṉiyār niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ tāmē muṉ vantu, kallātār irukkumiṭam tāma ceṉṟu, avarkaḷ ōyvu peṟum nēram aṟintu, avarkaḷ maṉa nilikkum maṉa niṟivukkum ēṟṟa vakiyil mella mella eḻuttaṟivittu ūkkuvikkum niliyiṉik kāṇkiṉṟōm. itaṉālēyē aṇṭi mānilamāṉa kēraḷattilē nūṟṟukku nūṟu āṇum peṇṇum eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟuc ciṟakkiṟārkaḷ eṉa aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭu ippōtu tāṉ anta vaḻiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟa niṉikkiṟatu.

kēraḷam pōṉṟa oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil-kuṟinta aḷavil kallātavar vāḻum pakutikaḷil kāṇum veṟṟiyiṉi nāṭṭu veṟṟiyākak koḷḷa muṭiyātu. kallāmākkaḷ peruvāriyāka uḷḷa pakutikaḷil taṉi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum aracum cērntu, perutta muyaṟciyilum celavilum pāṭupaṭṭāl oru vēḷi veṟṟi kāṇalāmō, eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. ēṟakkuṟiya nūṟu āṇṭukaḷākak kēraḷamum-micōrāmum iṭiyaṟātu pāṭupaṭṭu niṉṟa kāraṇattiṉālētāṉ aṅkellām niṟi nalamāka mutiyōr kalvi veṟṟi peṟa, kallātār illiyeṉap pōṟṟap peṟuvatu aṟikiṟōm (p. 200). attakiya muyaṟciyiṉi ellā mānilaṅkaḷum mēṟkoṇṭāl virivil nāṭṭil kallātār illiyāvar. ām! kaṟṟa āṇum peṇṇum akkallā maṉitaṉuṭaṉō peṇṇuṭaṉō paḻaki niṉṟu, avarkaḷ maṉanili aṟintu mella mellak kalviyiṉ avaciyattip pukaṭṭi ciṟu ciṟu vakiyil eḻuttaṟivittu, avartam moḻi vaḷarkkum neṟiyil payiṟṟuvittu, tām cōrvu aṭiyāmal taḷarcciyuṟāmal payiṟṟāḷarākac ceyalpurivarāyiṉ niccayamākap payaṉ viḷiyum. iṅkē irāmamūrtti kuḻu inta mutiyōr kalvi vaḷarat tanta cila karuttukkaḷi appaṭiyē uṅkaḷmuṉ vikkiṉṟēṉ avi aṉittum, eṉṉālō-maṟṟavar aṉivarālumō ēṟṟukkoḷḷak kūṭiyavi eṉṟu nāṉ koḷḷāviṭiṉum ikkaruttu ceyalāṟṟuvōrukkut tūṇṭukōlāka amiyum eṉpatu eṉ tuṇivu.

RECOMMENDATIONS

Imperting of literacy should be placed in the context of the developmental needs of the adult. Adult education programmes should be accompanied by a wide range of measures relating to health, nutrition, housing, and employment needs. They should also address themselves in issues of fundamental rights, laws, secularism and democracy. After creating awareness in respect of these essentlal needs and issues the adult learner himself should be expected to ask for adult literacy, the stary should be in respect of creation of awareness for essential needs and from there work backward to adult literacy. ii) While the mass campign strategy may be tried out further, the alternative model being employed by the Department of Education through Mahila Samakhya may be closely monitored and its application for adult literacy examined-considering that the objective of this project is to create awareness regarding issues of survival and there by generate felt needs. iii) The mass campign strategy as well as the Mahila Samakhya model may be objectively evaluated to look for meanigful lessons for the future. iv) On most of the developmental problems and matters, realting to fundamental rights, social justice etc. the majority of the illiterates more often than not find themselves in positions of conflict with the official authorities. Therofore genuine initiatives for adult - education programmes. voluntary agencies, community groups, political parties and their mass organisations should be facilitated. v) While NLM goes ahead with its planned literacy campign in the Eighth Five Year Plan period, an independent study group should be commissioned to evaluate the programme, particularly with a view to arrive at an understanding of what may be appropriate strategies to remove adult illiteracy in the quicker possible time . The evalution may also look into the various alternative models and study their relevance with respect to diverse sociocultural and political conditions in different parts of India. The mininum objective basis what approaches do not yield results, so that, five years later, at least those models may not be encouraged. vi) The Department of Education should coordinate with the Department of Rurel Development and Ministry of Labout and organise programmes for vocational skills for the adult illiterates—facilitating flow of funds from programmes like TRYSEM (Trining of Youth for Self Employment). Community Polytechnics should also be involved in a large scale in imparting vocational skills amongst the adult illiterates. (This will enhance. the employability of the adult illiterates and thereby create awareness regarding basic needs and issues of life, in the process generating demand for adult literacy as a felt need ) vii) The non-literates should be placed in an environment in which they have constant interface with the challenge of the written word. viii) Illiterate adults are those who have either not had access to education or having had access, have been unable to complete their schooling. A person has to remin in school atleast for a minimum of four years to attin a relatively irreversible level of iiteracy. Literacy should be a form of basic trining making it possible for the adult to acquire some knowledge as may be necessary. It is imperative that Universalisation of Elementary Education is given top priority in educational planning and resource allocation. The objective should be to ensure that no child in the early nineties shall grow into an illiterate adult in. the next century. (If this can be achieved, we would have then succeeded in controlling the chief contributor to illiteracy in India, i.e. low rate of participation in school education.) virumpum nili vaḷarntu varukiṟatu. intiya nāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ aṉittum añcal vaḻik kalviyi aṟimukappaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. itu paṟṟi muṉṉarē ōraḷavu kaṇṭōm. palkalik kaḻakap paṭṭap paṭippiṉiyum uyarpaṭṭap paṭippiṉiyum añcal vaḻiyē kaṟṟut tarukiṉṟaṉa. ataṟkeṉa ovvoru palkalik kaḻakamum taṉittuṟiyiṉiyē amittup pāṭaṅkaḷit tayārittu aṉuppukiṉṟaṉa. oru cila muṟiyāka iyaṅka, cila tavaṟukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭam peṟa eṇṇip pala vakiyil kaṉavu kāṇum palar allaluṟavum kāṇkiṟōm. pāṭaṅkaḷi muṟiyāka aṉuppuvatum iṭiyiṭiyē kuṟitta iṭaṅkaḷil pala āciriyarkaḷik koṇṭu pāṭaṅkaḷ naṭattuvatum muṟiyāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum paṇipuriyum āṭavarum makaḷirum ivaṟṟil cērntu payaṉpeṟa virumpukiṉṟaṉar; peṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum āyntu kaṇṭavarkaḷ ivarkaḷ taram muṟiyākap payilupavar tarattiṉum tāḻntē uḷḷatu eṉpar. āṇṭoṉṟukku 80 nāḷkaḷ (nāḷikku 5 maṇi nēram) nallāciriyarkaḷ kīḻē amarntu palvēṟu pāṭaṅkaḷi muṟiyākap payiṉṟu, paruva iṭit tērvu, kālāṇṭu,ariyāṇṭut tērvukaḷ eḻuti tiruttap peṟṟu, taṅkaḷ tavaṟukaḷi avvappōtu uṇarntu tiruntum māṇavarkaḷōṭu ivarkaḷi oppiṭuvatu poruntātatutāṉ eṉiṉum ivarkaḷum muyaṉṟu, payiṉṟu veṟṟi peṟukiṉṟami pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatākum. āyiṉum orucila palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ palariyum tērcciyuṟac ceytāltāṉ nam palkalik kaḻakattukku atika māṇavar varuvar-atika varuvāy kiṭikkum eṉṟa eṇṇattil tavaṟukaḷ iḻippatākak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. mēlum eṅkō oruvar pāṭaṅkaḷit tayārikka, vēṟu eṅkō oruvar āṇṭukku ōriru muṟi pāṭam naṭatta, eppōtō kaṭum kōṭiyil orē muṟi tērvuk kaḷattil kutikkum ivarkaḷ nili eṇṇattakka oṉṟākum. mēlum āyvukkaḷat toṭarpuṭiya paṭṭaṅkaḷiyum āciriyar payiṟcik kalvi pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum inta añcal vaḻik kalvikku appālē irukka vittal nalamākum. intak koḷkiyil palkalik kaḻaka māṉiyak kuḻu virivil cila cīrtiruttaṅkaḷic ceyalpaṭutta niṉikkum kuṟippukaḷ nāḷitaḻil veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa. taṟpōtu tiṟantaveḷip palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. cila palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ ataṟkeṉat taṉittuṟikaḷi amittuḷḷaṉa. paṭippē aṟiyātavarō aṉṟic ciṟitu payiṉṟavarō nērē paṭṭat tērvukkuc cella vaḻi uṇṭu. cila palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ aṭippaṭip paṭippi valiyuṟuttum oru nuḻivut tērvil veṟṟi peṟutal oṉṟum pōtumāṉatu, itu avvaḷavu ciṟantatā? avaṟṟuḷ payiṉṟōr uṇmiyil kaṟṟavarkaḷ āvārkaḷā eṉpati varum kālamtāṉ namakku uṇarttum, eppaṭiyō nāṭṭil eḻuttaṟiyātavarkaḷi eḻuttaṟivu uḷḷavarkaḷākkutaṟkum caṟṟē paṭittavarkaḷ mēlum mēlum paṭittup paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvataṟkum palappala putiya muṟikaḷ vakukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittum cemmiyākac ceyalāṟṟiṉ niṉitta payaṉip peṟa iyalum, iṉṟēl kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa nāṭṭup paṇam celavāvatōṭu kālamum vīṇē kaḻintoḻintatāka muṭiyum. eṉavē ittakiya kalvi niliyaṅkaḷik kaṭṭik kākkum poṟuppuḷḷavarkaḷ nāṭṭukkum camutāyattukkum tām āṟṟa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta-tēviyāṉa cēvikaḷi maṉattil koṇṭu nāṭṭuk kalviyē mukkiyam eṉṟa uṇarvil ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ. ellārum kalvi kaṟṟu, mēlum kaṟṟu, mēṉmēlum kaṟṟu nāṭṭiṉ nalaṉik kaṭṭik kākka vēṇṭum eṉpatē eṉ āci!





8. toḻiṟ kalvi



* * *



toḻiṟkalvi urimi peṟṟapiṉ, caṟṟē vaḷarntirukkiṟatu eṉpati ōraḷavu ēṟṟuk koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. mattiya mānila aracukaḷ ittuṟiyil karuttiruttic ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collalām. tamiḻakac caṭṭamaṉṟattilē virivil toḻiltuṟiyil tamiḻnāṭu mutaliṭam peṟum eṉa mutalvar kūṟiyuḷḷatākac ceytit. tāḷvaḻi aṟiya muṭintatu. mattiya aracum taṉ nēraṭip pārviyilē nāṭṭiṉ tēvikkēṟṟa vakiyil niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷip pala mānilaṅkaḷil amittu varuvatu eṇṇat takkatu. eṉiṉum paranta pāratattukku inta vaḷarcci pōtātu eṉpatē aṟiñartam muṭivu. ataṟku ēṟpat toḻil evvaḷavu vaḷarcciyaṭiyiṉum ataṉ vaḻiyē uruvākum poruḷkaḷiṉa vili nāḷukku nāḷ uyarntu koṇṭētāṉ pōkiṟatu. itu vaḷarum nāṭṭukku. ēṟṟatākumā?

eṉ iḷamik kālattilē (1925-28) āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu vakuppukaḷil nāṉ payiṉṟapōtu, ētāvatu oru toḻilik kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟi iruntatu. nāṉ payiṉṟa vālājāpāt intumata pāṭacāliyil payirt toḻil, pāy neytal, tuṇi necavu, nūl nūṟṟal, pottāṉ ceytal, taccavēli, kayiṟu tirittuk kūṭi mutaliyaṉa ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷ kaṟṟuttarap peṟṟaṉ. māṇavaṉ ētēṉum oru toḻilit tavaṟātu kaṟṟuk koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. appaṭiyē 1936il nāṉ paṇiyāṟṟat toṭaṅkiya kāñcipuram āntiracaṉ uyarnilippaḷḷiyilum cila ciṟu kittoḻilkaḷ pāṭat tiṭṭattil iṭam peṟṟiruntami niṉivukku varukiṟatu. pattām vakuppu aṉṟi, aṉṟiya patiṉōrām vakuppu variyil payilum māṇavaṉ. ētēṉum oru toḻilik kalviyuṭaṉ kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpatu kaṭṭaḷi-muṟi. eṉiṉum kaṭṭāyam illiyātalāl pala paḷḷikaḷ ivaṟṟi ēṟṟu naṭattavilli. nam nāṭṭil aṉṟum iṉṟum etuvum kaṭṭāyamākkiṉāltāṉ naṭakkumpōlum. eṉiṉum mēlē 11ām vakuppukaḷil taṭṭeḻuttu pōṉṟavi toḻil amippil iṭampeṟa, paḷḷi iṟutit tērvil aviyum pāṭamāka ēṟkappeṟṟup pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē antiyar āṭci irunta anta nāḷilēyē paḷḷiyil payilum māṇavar ēṭṭuk kalviyuṭaṉ nāṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭum toḻiṟkalvi oṉṟiṉik muṟiyākap payila vaḻivaki iruntatu. āṉāl urimi peṟṟapiṉ anta nili illi eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. perumpālum 6 mutal 11vatu variyil payilupavar ittakiya toḻil oṉṟiṉik kaṟṟukkoṇṭāl, avar paḷḷippaṭippu muṭintatum vēli tēṭi aliyāml vīṭṭilēyē ētēṉum toḻil ceytu vāḻa vaḻi iruntatu inta marapu ēṉō urimi peṟṟa pāratattil otukkap peṟṟatu? -

irājāji mutalvarāka irunta kālattil toḻil kalviyiṉic cāti aṭippaṭiyil cārtti avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ cātikkamitta toḻilik kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉṟu viti amittār eṉṟum itaṉālēyē atu pala periya etirppukkaḷiṭiyē eṭupaṭāmal viṭupaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. paḷḷikaḷil pala toḻilkaḷi amittu, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiya toḻilip payilalām eṉa vitittu, avaṟṟiṟkut tērvum naṭattic cāṉṟitaḻ tara ēṟpāṭu ceytiruppārāyiṉ attoḻilmuṟi ōraḷavu nāṭṭil vaḷarntirukkum. ‘varuṇācirama’ marapu eṉa atu otukkappaṭṭatu eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ.

ka.-8 1974il putiya kalvi muṟi (10+2+3) pukuttiyapōtu mēnili vakuppukaḷil (11 & 12) toḻil pirivu eṉṟu (Vocational Course) oṉṟu amikkappeṟṟatu. aṉṟu anta mēnilippaḷḷik kuḻuvil nāṉ uṟuppiṉarāka iruntapōtu, iti valiyuṟuttip pala toḻilkaḷi ēṟpaṭutti, oṉṟiṉi māṇavar paṟṟip paṭara vaḻivakukkumāṟu kūṟiṉēṉ. eṉiṉum atu kāla kaṭṭattil veṟum taṭṭaccum curukkeḻuttum eṉṟa vakiyil amintatu. atilum pala māṇavar paṅku koṇṭaṉar. 1987-88 mēnili vakuppil payiṉṟa cumār 3.5 ilaṭcam māṇavaril 96,000 pēr (24%) intat toḻiṟkalvi payiṉṟār eṉa aracāṅkak kaṇakku tarappeṟukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum itil payilpavarkaḷ kallūriyil cēra vāyppu illāta nili uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kallūriyil cērkkum māṇavaril nūṟṟukkup pattu māṇavariyē cērkka viti amittaṉar pōlum. pala kallūrikaḷ atiyum piṉpaṟṟuvatilli. eṉavē ataṉ vaḷarcciyum taṭippaṭṭatu eṉalām. nāṉ muṉṉarē kāṭṭiyapaṭi ivarkaḷi aracāṅka eḻuttar patavikku mutaṉili tantu eṭuttuk koḷḷal poruntuvatākum. ippaṭiyē immēnili vakuppukaḷil āciriyar payiṟci vakuppum iruntatu. atil payiṉṟavarkaḷi-mēlum attuṟiyilēyē ōrāṇṭu īrāṇṭu payiṟci peṟacceytu, paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikka vaḻi ceytirukkalām. avvāṟillāmal avarkaḷum nāṟcantiyil niṉṟu naliya vēṇṭi vantamiyiṉ antat tuṟiyum aṇmiyil mūṭappaṭṭatu eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. ivvāṟu ākka neṟikkeṉa vakukkappeṟṟa pātikaḷ aracāṅka ūkkutal illāta kāraṇattiṉālēyē aḻikkappaṭum ōr avalanili nāṭṭil, kāṇa varunta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. toḻiṟkalvi eṉṟāl ētō poṟiyiyal, maruttuvam ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aminta paḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷil payilutal eṉṟē niṉittirukkiṟōm. cātāraṇa kuṭicit toḻilkaḷukkum payiṟciyum pātukāppum tēvi eṉpati maṟantu viṭukiṟōm. nāṉ muṉpu kāṭṭiya mutiyōr kalvi neṟiyil ittakiya toḻiṟkalviyum iṭampeṟiṉ, itik kaṟṟuk koḷḷuvatōṭu varupavarkaḷ ēṭṭuk kalviyilum payiṟci peṟa vāyppu uṇṭu. paḻaṅkālattil iruntatu pōṉṟu paḷḷiyilēyē-ciṟappākak kirāmappuṟa paḷḷikaḷilēyē kit toḻilkaḷ kaṟpikkappeṟal vēṇṭum, peruñcelvarkaḷip perum kittoḻil peruka ūkki ‘uḷḷavaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭum; illātavaṉiṭattiliruntu uḷḷatum eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭum’ eṉṟapaṭi, nāṭṭu naṭappum ceyal muṟiyum piṟavum iruntāl eppaṭit toḻil vaḷarum: eppaṭi ‘ellārum ellāc celvamum peṟṟu’ vāḻa muṭiyum? āḷupavar ivaṟṟiyellām cintittu uṭaṉ takka vakiyil ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. nāṭu vāḻa atuvē ciṟantatu. iṉṟu ciṟutoḻil vaḷarccikku ūkkamum ākkamum tara aracu muṉvarukiṟatu. ataṟkeṉa amiccum taṉittuṟiyum kūṭa uḷḷaṉa eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. āṉāl ataṟku māṉiyamākat tarappeṟum aracāṅkap paṇam ententa vakiyilō-vaḻaṅkupavarukkuk kiyūṭṭu, piṟavakic celavukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭukiṟatu eṉpar.

nāṭṭil toḻiṟkalvi ōṅki varukiṟateṉiṉum pōtiya aḷavil vaḷarcci aṭiyavilli. intiyak kalvikkuḻu itupaṟṟit taṉ karuttiṉit terivittu (National Policy of Education) ataṉ vaḷarccikku vaḻikāṇa muyalkiṉṟatu. tiru. irāmamūrtti kuḻuvum ataṉ karuttukkaḷi ārāyntu vaḷarccikku vaḻikāṭṭiyuḷḷatu.

tamiḻnāṭu uṭpaṭa nāṉku mānilaṅkaḷil urimam peṟṟum peṟāmalum pala poṟiyiyaṟ kallūrikaḷum toḻiṟ paḷḷikaḷum iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum avaṟṟuḷ pala tarattil tāḻntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum akkuṟippu kāṭṭukiṟatu. ām! tamiḻnāṭṭil aracāṅkam ceṉṟa āṇṭu oru kallūriyōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, vēṟupāṭuṟṟa niliyiṉi nāṭaṟiyum. mēlum āḷupavarukku uṟṟavarākavum palkalik kaḻakattukkup palavakiyil vēṇṭiyavarākavum iruppiṉ poṟiyiyaṟ kallūri maṭṭumaṉṟi, putitāka entak kallūriyum toṭaṅka vāyppu uḷḷati nāṭṭu naṭavaṭikkikaḷ naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟaṉavē. māṟimāṟi varum āḷupavar kippāvikaḷākap pala kallūrikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa-avi ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiya niliyil māṇavarkaḷit tiṟaṉaṟintavarkaḷāka ākkum vakiyil irukkiṉṟaṉavā eṉpatu aiyattukkiṭamāka uḷḷatē. maṟṟum peṇkaḷukkuc camurimi eṉṟu mēṭiyilum caṭṭamaṉṟattilum muḻaṅkum niliyilum toḻiṟ. kallūriyil 12% cāṉṟitaḻ paḷḷikaḷil 17% tāṉ payilkiṉṟaṉar. cīrmarapiṉar aṉṟi otukkappaṭṭavar paṭṭap paṭippil 5% cāṉṟitaḻ paḷḷiyil 9% uḷḷaṉar (pa 237). inta avala niliyil makaḷir muṉṉēṟṟamum tāḻntōr uyarvatum muyaṟkompu-ākāyappūp pōṉṟatākum.

ittakiya toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷ urimam peṟṟavi peṟātavi naṉku iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉavā eṉakkāṇal araciṉ kaṭamiyākum. nalla āyvuk kūṭaṅkaḷ, nallāciriyarkaḷ, nalla kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ illāmalēyē pala iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉap pēcukiṉṟaṉar-aṟikki viṭukiṉṟaṉar. aracō palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷō inta aval nilikaḷip paṟṟi eṇṇuvatilli pōlum. vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉ oṉṟum illāmalē ellā vakuppukaḷiyum tarutalum vēṇṭātavarāyiṉ ellām iruntum ētō kāraṇam kāṭṭi oṉṟum tarātu oḻippatum palkalik kaḻakac ceyalāka amivatu eṉkiṉṟaṉar.

poṟiyiyal, maruttuvattuṟi pōṉṟavaṟṟilum kālnaṭi maruttuvam, vēḷāṇmi pōṉṟavaṟṟilum takka aṉupavam mikkavarkaḷiyē āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikka vēṇṭum. iṅkēyum patavi uyarvu mutaliyavaṟṟilēyum iṉṉār iṉiyār eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭappeṟukiṟatu. eṉpar. aṭippaṭit tēviyāṉa cātaṉaṅkaḷ-karuvikaḷ kūṭa illāmal eppaṭi ivi iyaṅka muṭiyum mattiya aracu ivaṟṟukkeṉa vaḻaṅkum perum tokikaḷi mānila aracu taṉ celavukaḷukkeṉa vēṟu vakiyil celavu ceytuviṭalām. itaṉāl muṟiyākac ceyalpaṭum paḷḷikaḷ kallūrikaḷi antarattil viṭa nēriṭukiṉṟatu. 1989-90 il muṭikkap peṟṟa paṇikaḷukkum ceymuṟikaḷukkum cila paḷḷikaḷukku 1990 iṭiyilēyē mattiya aracu muḻu māṉiyat tokiyiṉi mānila, aracukku aṉuppiyum, iṉṉum attokikaḷ uriya kalvi nilikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu cēravilli eṉkiṉṟaṉar. piṉ avi evvāṟu naṉku ceyalpaṭum? vēliyē payiri, mēyum vakiyil ivvāṟu pala māṉiyaṅkaḷ iṭiyil aracāṅkattālē avalamākkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟiya aracu itikkaṇṭu āvaṉa ceyyum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ.

kirāma makkaḷi īṭupaṭuttum vakiyil palkalit toḻiṟ paḷḷikaḷ (Polytecnics) iyaṅkavēṇṭum. pallāyirakkaṇakkil paṇam koṭuttāltāṉ iṭam kiṭikkum eṉṟa nili māṟavēṇṭum. kaṇṇiruntum kuruṭarāy-kātiruntum ceviṭarāy aṉittiyum kaṇṭu vāḷāliruntāl, nāṭu iṉṉum iḻiniliyittāṉ aṭiyum. ivaṟṟi eṇṇi nāṭāḷum nallavarum patavi vakikkum paṭṭatārikaḷum aluvalarkaḷum piṟarum nāṭṭu nilaṉilē akkaṟi koṇṭu ceyalpaṭutal naṉṟākum. mukkiyamākat toḻiṟkalvi vaḷara aṉivarum oṉṟic ceyalāṟṟiṉāltāṉ payaṉ uṇṭu. camutāyattōṭu toṭarpuṭiya toḻilkaḷi ūrtōṟum amittu; avaṟṟiṟkut toḻiliyal cāṉṟitaḻ allatu paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷit takkavāṟu amittu, vēṇṭiya upkaraṇaṅkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum tantu aracu ūkka vēṇṭum. kirāmaṅkaḷil payiriṭuvōr pāti nēratti vīṇākkāmal nāṭṭukkut tēviyāṉa poruḷkaḷi uṟpatti ceytutarum nili vaḷara vēṇṭum. paṭittu viṭṭu vēli illātirukkum niliyum itaṉāl illiyākum ellārum nakariltāṉ uttiyōkam pārkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nili iruntāl pala kōṭiyāka vāḻum nam nāṭṭuk kirāma makkaḷ kati eṉṉāvatu? toḻil mayam kirāmaṅkaḷil toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.

toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷit toṭaṅkuvataṟkup palkalik kaḻakam, mānila aracu, mattiya aracu ākiya aṉittiliruntum icivu peṟutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. vēṇṭuvōrtam tēviyiṉi aṟintuvarum viṇṇappaṅkaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ kavaṉittu, toṭaṅkuvataṟku mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷukku muṉṉarē muṭivu eṭuttu mattiya, mānila aracukaḷum palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum āṇi aṉuppiṉāl naṉ māṇavarkaḷ cērkkap peṟṟup payaṉaṭivar. eṉavē putiya toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷ tiṟakkavum putiya pāṭaṅkaḷ toṭaṅkavum varum viṇṇappaṅkaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ ārāyntu iru aracukaḷum palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭiya kaṭamiyiṉi maṟattalākātu. icivu vaḻaṅkuvatilum nēril tiṟaṉaṟi kuḻukkaḷ amittu nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭu amippāḷarkaḷ takka vakiyil tēviyāṉavaṟṟic ceytirukkiṟārkaḷā eṉak kaṇṭē icivu vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum. virintu ceyalpaṭiṉ viṭivu uṇṭu viḷivum uṇṭu.

toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷip pala toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷōṭu iṇikkum vakiyil amikka vēṇṭum. veṟum ēṭṭup paṭippum aṅkē ceyyum cila kaḷa āyvukaḷum pōtā! ‘ēṭṭuk kalvi kavikku utavātu’ eṉpatu ellāk kalvikkum poruntum eṉṟālum toḻiṟ kalvikku mika mikamukkiyamāṉatu. ‘ēṭṭuc curakkāy kaṟikku utavātu’ eṉṟa maṟṟoru paḻamoḻiyum itaṟkup poruntum. maruttuvakkallūrikku uṭaṉ iṇintē ovvoru maruttuvamaṉi irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti iruppatu pōṉṟu poṟiyiyal pōṉṟa toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷukkum ōr uyariya toḻiṟcāli allatu toḻiṟpēṭṭi irukka vēṇṭum eṉa viti ceyya vēṇṭum. payilum māṇavar pāti nērattilō-aṉṟit tēviyāṉa nērattilō attoḻiṟcāliyil nēriya payiṟci peṟal vēṇṭum, nāṉ muṉṉē kirāmaṅkaḷukkuk kāṭṭiya ciṟutoḻil neṟippaṭi, intak kallūrikaḷil paṭikkum māṇavar peruntoḻilō ciṟu toḻilō tēviyāṉāl kirāmantōṟum tāmē toṭaṅka untutal peṟṟavarātal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl palakōṭi toḻiṟkalvikkuc celaviṭutalāl payaṉilli, aracāṅkamum toḻiṟ kallūri amip pōrum inta vakiyil karuttirutti āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭumeṉakkēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ.

toḻiṟ kalviyiṉ ovvoru tuṟiyiṉiyum taṉit taṉi ārāyiṉ atuvē perunūlākum. ataṟku ēṟṟa taṉit takutiyum eṉakku illi eṉpati nāṉ naṉku aṟivēṉ. maruttuvam, poṟiyiyal, kaṇippoṟi, ciṟu toḻilkaḷ aṉittilum avvattuṟiyil vallavarkaḷit taṉittaṉiyāka mānila miya aracukaḷ aḻittu, makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟa pakutikku ēṟṟa takka payaṉ viḷiyattakka vakiyil ceyalpāṭukaḷum amiyum vakiyil pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum payiṟṟu muṟiyiṉiyum kaḷappaṇikaḷiyum amittu, atiyum cari pārkka ōr uyarnilik kuḻuviyum ovvoru tuṟikkum amittu āvaṉa kāṇiṉ nāṭu nalampeṟum. eppōtō orumuṟi eṅkō talinakaril kuḷukuḷu aṟiyil uṭkārntu koṇṭu tiṭṭam tīṭṭiṉāl payaṉilli. iṭantōṟum, ceṉṟu-ūrtōṟum nilavum nili kaṇṭu avvannilikku ēṟṟavakiyil paḷḷi, kallūrikaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷi amittu avaṟṟip pōṟṟip pātutākka vēṇṭum. nāṉ mēlēkāṭṭiyapaṭi, avvattuṟikaḷukku ēṟṟa toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ iṇikkappeṟal vēṇṭum; aṉṟi amikkappeṟa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟaṉṟi veṟum toḻiṟ paḷḷi, kallūrikaḷi maṭṭum toṭaṅki viṭṭāl maṟṟoru vēli illāp paṭṭāḷamē uruvākum. mēlum nāṭṭil pala cikkalkaḷum koṭumikaḷum piṟa iḻinilikaḷum tōṉṟa vaḻivakukkum. nāṭāḷum nallavarkaḷ ivaṟṟiyellām eṇṇic ceyalāṟṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ nāṭu nalam peṟum. nām ulakattil uyarvōm. uṇmi vāḻvum nalamum malarum.

intat toḻiṟkalvi paṟṟit tiru. irāmamūrtti kuḻuviṉar āyntu teḷinta muṭivukaḷi neṟippaṭutta vēṇṭip parinturi ceytuḷḷaṉar (pak. 243-248). ovvoru vakiyiṉiyum naṉku ārāyntu ataṟku eṉṉeṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉa vakippaṭuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ittuṟiyil paṇipurivōr aṉivarumē ivaṟṟi neṟi aṟintu pōṟṟal vēṇṭum. intap parinturikaḷē muṭivu eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉa nāṉ kūṟamāṭṭēṉ. ivi muṉṉēṟṟap paṭikaḷuḷ cila. teḷiyat teḷiya, ārāya ārāya ulaka vaḷarccikku oppa nam nāṭṭut toḻiṟkalviyum ataṟkeṉa aminta piṟa tuṇinilik karuvikaḷum iṭamum kālamum piṟavum amiyap pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. cila nāṭukaḷ muyalvatu pōṉṟu aṟiviyal neṟi aḻivuppātikku vaḻi kōlāmal ākkap pātikku vaḻi kōluvatāy-ulakac camutāya vāḻvukku ukantatāy amiya vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum. yāṇṭum iṉpam niṟiya-ellārum ellāc celvamum peṟa-viyakam vaḷamuṟa-kalvit tuṟiyiṉ ellāppakutikaḷum muyaṉṟu orumi uṇarvil ceyalāṟṟiṉ nāṭum ulakamum, uyyum uyarum annāḷ virintu varuvatāka!

nalla āciriyariṉ ilakkaṇam

kulaṉ aruḷ teyvaṅ koḷki mēṉmi

kalipayil teḷivu kaṭṭuri vaṉmi

nilam, mali niṟikōl, malarnikar māṭciyum

ulakiyal aṟivōṭu uyar kuṇam iṇiyavum

amipavaṉ nūl uri āciriyaṉṉē.



(naṉṉūl)





pāṭam collum muṟi

ītal iyalpē iyampum kāli

kālamum iṭaṉum vālitiṉ nōkkic

ciṟantuḻi iruntutaṉ teyvam vāḻtti

urikkap paṭuporuḷ uḷḷat tamittu

viriyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu

koḷvōṉ koḷvaki aṟintu avaṉ uḷaṅkoḷak

kōṭṭamil maṉattiṉ nūlkoṭuttal eṉpa.



(naṉṉūl)





pāṭamkēṭkum muṟi

kōṭalmarapē kūṟum kālip

poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭal muṉiyāṉ

kuṇattoṭu paḻaki avaṉkuṟippiṉ cārntu

irueṉa iruntu coleṉac collip

parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvattiṉ ākic

cittirap pāviyiṉ attakavu aṭaṅkic

cevi vāyāka neñcu kaḷaṉākak

kēṭṭavi kēṭṭavi viṭātu uḷattu amittup

pō eṉap pōtal eṉmaṉār pulavar.



(naṉṉūl)





inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:

Guruleninn

Balajijagadesh

Sgvijayakumar

Kaarunya shri.k
